puṟappāṭṭiṟ kāṇalāvatoru ceyti im maṉṉaṟkup periya maṟuviṉiyuṇṭākkuva teṉpatil aiyamilli. acceytiyāvatu, variyāta vaḷḷaṇmiyāl ulakamuḷḷaḷavum aḻiyāta pukaḻiṉip paṭitta kaṭiyeḻu vaḷḷalkaḷil oruvaṉum, tirukkōvalūri aracirukkiyāka vuṭiyavaṉumākiya maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāriyiṉ mikka iḷamiyuṭiya makkaḷip paṟṟik koṇarntu yāṉikkālil iṭaṟuvikkat tuṇintamiyē yākum. ivaṉ aṅṅaṉam ceṟṟaṅ koṇṭamikkuk kāraṇam ituvākumeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu; kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ pukāriliruntu aracāṇṭapoḻutu neṭuṅkiḷḷi eṉṉum cōḻamaṉṉaṉ uṟiyūrit talinakarākak koṇṭu āṇṭu vantaṉaṉ. kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉukkum neṭuṅkiḷḷikkum pakimi mūṇṭatu. kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ tampiyum iḷavaracaṉumākiya nalaṅkiḷḷi neṭuṅkiḷḷi yuṭaṉ pala iṭaṅkaḷiṟ porutu, muṭivil kariyāṟu eṉṉu miṭattil naṭanta pōrilē avaṉikkoṉṟaṉaṉ. appoḻutu neṭuṅkiḷḷikkut tuṇiyāka aṅku vantetirtta cēra pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷum nalaṅkiḷḷiyāl muṟiyaṭikkappaṭṭaṉar. kāriyāṟṟil ivinikaḻntami, neṭuṅkiḷḷiyāṉavaṉ kāriyāṟṟuttuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷi eṉa vaḻaṅkap paṭutalāṉum,
"	vañciyi ṉiruntu vañci cūṭi
...	...	...
talittārc cēṉiyoṭu malittut talivantōr
cilikkayal neṭuṅkoṭi ceruvēṟ ṟaṭakki
ārpuṉi teriyal iḷaṅkōṉ ṟaṉṉāl
kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa kāval veṇkuṭi
valikeḻu taṭakki māvaṇ kiḷḷi"
eṉak kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭutalāṉum  pulaṉākiṉṟatu. iṅṅaṉam neṭuṅkiḷḷiyi veṉṟapiṉpu uṟiyūrum kiḷḷivaḷavaṟkum nalaṅkiḷḷikkum uriyatāyiṟṟu. ataṉāṟṟāṉ,
"	piṟaṅkunili māṭat tuṟanti yōṉē"
eṉak kiḷḷivaḷavaṉum,
"uṟanti yōṉē kuricil"
eṉa nalaṅkiḷḷiyum puṟappāṭṭukkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar.itu niṟka. - tirumuṭikkāriyāṉavaṉ peruvaḷḷalāka viḷaṅkiyataṉṟi, oppaṟṟa vīraṉākavum tikaḻntāṉ. ataṉāl, mūvēntarkaḷ tamakkuḷḷē pōr nērnta kāliyil kārikkup perum poruḷ koṭuttu avaṉit tamakkup paṭit tuṇiyākumpaṭi taṉittaṉi vēṇṭuvar. kāriyum taḷarntavar pakkaltuṇiyākac ceṉṟu porutu, tāṉpeṟṟa poruḷaṉittiyum paricilarkku vaḻaṅkiviṭuvāṉ. itaṉi,
"	vīyāt tiruviṉ viṟalkeḻu tāṉi
mūvaru ḷoruvaṉ ṟuppā kiyareṉa	
ēttiṉar taruuṅ kūḻē nuṅkuṭi
vāḻttiṉar varūum iravala ratuvē" 	(puṟam, 122)
eṉpataṉāl aṟiyalākum. icceyal kārikkē yaṉṟi avaṉ kuṭiyiṟ piṟantār piṟarukkum iyalpāyiruntatu. kāriyāṉavaṉ neṭuṅkiḷḷikkut tuṇipurintamiyālō, avaṉ makkaḷu
ḷoruvaṉ neṭuṅkiḷḷiyiṉ makaṉukku aracuniliyiṭa muṉin
tamiyālō kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukku maliyamāṉ makkaḷiṭattut takutiyillāta cīṟṟamuṇṭāyatu. ikkoṭuñ ceyal nikaḻvuḻi aṅku vantirunta kōvūrkiḻār eṉṉum pulavar kaṇṭu pati
patittu iraṅki, aracaṉi nōkki 'nī tāṉ, ōr puṟāviṉ allalaṉṟiyum piṟavuyirkaḷukkuṟṟa tuṉpattiyum tīrttaṟku uyirkkoṭi pūṇṭa cōḻaṉ marapiluḷḷāy; ivar tām, aṟivāl uḻutuṇṇum kaṟṟōratu vaṟumit tuṉpattiṟku añcit tammuṭiya poruḷip pakuttuṇṇum taṇṇiya aruḷiyuṭiyāratu marapiluḷḷār; ivar ippoḻutu kaḷiṟṟik
kaṇṭa viyappāl, muṉpu veruviyaḻuta aḻukiyi maṟantiruk
kiṉṟa ciṟu piḷḷikaḷ; immaṉṟiṉi veruṇṭu nōkki muṉpu aṟiyāta putiyatōr varuttattiyum uṭiyar; itaṉik kēṭṭaṉiyāyiṉ nī virumpiyatic ceyka' eṉṉum poruḷaminta ōr pāṭṭiṉik kūṟiṉar. itaṉikkēṭṭa kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ taṉ kuṟṟattiṟku nāṇi, appiḷḷikaḷik kollātu viṭuttaṉaṉ. vaḷavaṉ mutaliṟ ceyyat tuṇinta kāriyampaṟṟi avaṉi ettuṇiyum eḷḷutal poruntumēṉum, piṉpu cāṉṟōr coṟkēṭṭu ataṉic ceyyātoḻintami karuti avaṉip pārāṭṭavē vēṇṭum.
iṉi, kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ tāṉ karuvūri muṟṟukiyiṭṭuc cēraṉi veṉṟatum, taṉ tampiyāl neṭuṅkiḷḷiyiyum, cēra pāṇṭiyarkaḷiyum veṉṟatumaṉṟi, tāṉ maturiyiṟ ceṉṟu paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉ eṉpāṉi veṉṟāṉeṉṟu ōr akappāṭṭiṉāl terikiṟatu. ivaṉ kūṭal maṟukilē paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉi veṉṟa poḻutu kōtimārpaṉ eṉpāṉ pēruvakiyuṟṟaṉaṉ eṉṟum appāṭṭuk kūṟukiṟatu.
iṉi, ivaṉukkuc ciṟukuṭikiḻāṉ paṇṇaṉ eṉpāṉ ciṟanta naṭpāḷaṉāyiruntāṉ. paṇṇaṉ mītu ivvaracar peruntaki pāṭiya ceyyuḷoṉṟu puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷatu. ac ceyyuḷi
liruntu paṇṇaṉuṭiya īkit taṉmiyum, avaṉpāl vaḷavaṉ pūṇṭa naṭpiṉ pāṉmiyum, vaḷavaṉatu kavipāṭuntiṟalum naṉku viḷaṅkum. acceyyuḷ,
"	yāṉvāḻu nāḷum paṇṇaṉ vāḻiya
pāṇar kāṇkivaṉ kaṭumpiṉa tiṭumpi
yāṇarp paḻumaram puḷḷimirn taṉṉa
ūṇoli yaravan tāṉuṅ kēṭkum
poyyā veḻili peyviṭam nōkki
muṭṭi koṇṭu vaṟpulañ cērum
ciṟu nuṇ ṇeṟumpiṉ cilloḻuk kēyppac
cōṟuṭik kiyar vīṟṟuvīṟ ṟiyaṅkum
iruṅkiḷic ciṟāark kāṇṭuṅ kaṇṭum	
maṟṟu maṟṟum viṉavun teṟṟeṉap
pacippiṇi maruttuva ṉillam
aṇittō cēyttō kūṟumi ṉemakkē"
eṉpatu paṇṇaṉiṭattiṟ paricil peṟṟu mīṇṭu varukiṉṟa pāṇaṉoruvaṉ avaṉpāṟ paricilpeṟutaṟkuc cuṟṟattuṭaṉ vantukoṇṭirukkiṟa pāṇaṉi vaḻiyiṟ kaṇṭu, avaṉ viṉāviya taṉik kēṭṭu, taṉ pakkappāṇari nōkkik kūṟiyatākap paṇṇaṉatu oppaṟṟa  īki pulappaṭap pāṭap peṟṟatu ippāṭṭu. itaṉ poruḷ; 'paḻutta marattiṉ kaṇṇē puḷḷiṉam olittāṟ pōṉṟa ūṇāluṇṭākiya āravārantāṉuṅ kēṭkum; maḻi peyyuṅ kālatti nōkkit tam muṭṭikaḷik koṇṭu mēṭṭu nilatti yaṭiyum ciṟṟeṟumpiṉ oḻukkuppōlac cōṟṟut tiraḷiyik kiyiluṭiyarāy vevvēṟu variciyākac celkiṉṟa periya cuṟṟattōṭuṅ kūṭiya piḷḷikaḷiyum kāṇkiṉṟēm; aṅṅaṉam kēṭṭum kaṇṭum, em paci varuttattāl, paci nōy tīrkkum maruttuvaṉatu maṉi aṇimiyiluḷḷatō cēymiyiṉuḷḷatō kūṟuṅkaḷ eṉap piṉṉum piṉṉum virintu viṉavukiṉṟēm; eṉṟu ippāṇaṉ kūṟukiṉṟāṉ; pāṇarē ipparicilaṉatu cuṟṟattiṉ vaṟumiyik kāṇpīrāka; iṅṅaṉam eṉ vaṟumiyum tīrttu ivaṉ vaṟumiyum tīrkka virukkiṉṟa paṇṇaṉ yāṉ uyir vāḻu nāḷiyum peṟṟu vāḻvāṉāka' eṉpatu. itiluḷḷa uvami nayam mutaliya aḻakukaḷ kaṟṟōr neñcip piṇikkun taṉmiyaṉa.
iṉi, kāvirippūm paṭṭiṉam ivaṉ kālattilēyē kaṭalāṟ koḷḷappaṭṭateṉṟum, ivaṉ vēṟiṭat tiyaṭintāṉeṉṟum maṇimēkali kūṟukiṟatu. ivaṉ aṭinta vēṟiṭam uṟiyūrō kāñcinakarō teriyavilli. pukār kaṭalkōṭpaṭṭapiṉ mātavi makaḷākiya maṇimēkali kāñcippatiyi aṭinta poḻutu annakarattil toṭukaḻaṟkiḷḷi yeṉṉum cōḻa ṉoruvaṉum avaṉukkut tampiyākat tuṇiyiḷaṅkiḷḷi yeṉpāṉum iruntārāka akkāppiyam kūṟukiṟatu. aṉṉār kiḷḷivaḷavaṉum avaṉ ṟampiyumāyiṉ avarkaḷ kāñciyi aṭintārāvar.
ivvēntaṉ muṭivil, cēra nāṭṭiluḷḷatākiya kuḷamuṟṟam eṉṉum iṭattil naṭanta pōril uyir tuṟantāṉāvaṉ. ataṉāl ivaṉ peyar, cōḻaṉ kuḷamuṟṟuttut tuñciya kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉ eṉap piṉpu vaḻaṅkuvatāyiṟṟu. ivaṉip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ, puṟattil, ālattūr kiḻār, veḷḷikkuṭi nākaṉār, māṟōkattu nappacaliyār, āvūr mūlaṅkiḻār, kōvūrkiḻār, iṭikkāṭaṉār, āṭutuṟi mācāttaṉār, aiyūr muṭavaṉār, nalliṟiyaṉār, erukkāṭṭūrt tāyaṅkaṇṇaṉār eṉpōrum, akattil, nakkīrarum āvar. ivaruḷ nappacaliyār peṇpāla rāyiruttal vēṇṭum. ivarkaḷil, ivvaracaṉ tuñciyapiṉṉarum iruntu pirivāṟṟātu varuntip pāṭiyavarkaḷ māṟōkattu nappacaliyārum, āṭutuṟi mācāttaṉārum, aiyūr muṭavaṉārum āvar. iṉṉōr iraṅkik kūṟiyavi neñci urukkun taṉmiyaṉa. nappacaliyār, 'poṉṉāṉiyaṉṟa māliyiṉiyum, neruṅkiya pōriṉkaṇ etirniṉṟu vellum paṭiyiṉiyum, tiṇṇiya tēriṉiyumuṭiya vaḷavaṉik koṇṭa kūṟṟu, taṉ maṉattuḷē kaṟuvu koṇṭatāyiṉum, eyti niṉṟu kiyōṭu mey tīṇṭi varuttiṟṟāyiṉum ataṟkup piḻittal illi; atu, pāṭuvār pōlat tōṉṟi niṉṟu kiyāṟṟoḻutu vāḻtti irantu uyirkoṇṭatākal vēṇṭum' eṉṟum; mācāttaṉār, 'īramillāta kūṟṟamē, nī mikavum aṟiviliyā kiṉṟāy; vāṭ pōril valla vīrarum yāṉiyum kutiriyum pōrkkaḷattil māya etirniṉṟu koṉṟu nāṭōṟum niṉatu paciyit tīrttaṟku ūṭṭiya vaciyaṟṟa valiyiyuṭiya vaḷavaṉi nī koṇṭāyāyiṉ iṉi niṉ paciyip pōkkuvōr yār colvāyāka; viraku illāmiyāl mēl viḷintu payaṉpaṭum vitiyik kuttiyuṇṭāy; niṉakku yāṉ kūṟiya ivvārtti piṟaḻāta meyyātali iṉṉamum kāṇpi' eṉṟum; kiyaṟu niliyākak kūṟiṉar.
muṭavaṉārō, cuṟṟattār iraṅkik kūṟum āṉantap piyuḷ eṉṉum tuṟiyil vittu, `kalam vaṉiyum vēṭkōvē, kalam vaṉiyum vēṭkōvē, iruḷ ōriṭattē ceṟintu niṉṟāṟ pōlat tiraṇṭa pukivāṉiṉkaṭ ceṉṟu poruntum cūḷiyi yuṭiya mūtūriṟ kalam vaṉiyum vēṭkōvē, nilamuḻutum parappiya periya tāṉiyiṉiyum, pulavar pukaḻnta poyyāta nalla pukaḻiṉiyum uṭiya cempiyar marukaṉākiya vaḷavaṉ tēvarulakatti aṭintāṉākalāṉ, attaṉmiyuṭi yōṉik kavikkum iṭamakaṉṟa tāḻiyi1 nī vaṉiya virumpiṉāyāyiṉ periya nilavaṭṭam tikiriyāka mērumalimaṇṇāka niṉakku vaṉiya iyalumō; iyalāmiyiṉ ettaṉmiyi yākuvi; nī iraṅkattakkāy' eṉṟu kūṟiṉar. iṅṅaṉam, kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ tuñciyapiṉ pāṭiya icceyyuṭkaḷiliruntu, nallicip pulavar
kaṭku avaṉ mēl ettuṇi aṉpiruntateṉpatu naṉku pulaṉākiṉṟatu.



11. nalaṅkiḷḷi
ivaṉ kiḷḷivaḷavaṉukkut tampi yeṉpatu `cilikkayal neṭuṅkoṭi ārpuṉiteriyal iḷaṅkōṉ ṟaṉṉāl kāriyāṟṟuk koṇṭa māvaṇ kiḷḷi' eṉak kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟappaṭuvatāl aṟiyalāvatu. ivaṉ tampi māvaḷattāṉ eṉpatu 43 ām puṟappāṭṭiṉ kīḻuḷḷa pāṭiṉōr pāṭappaṭṭōrik kuṟikkum toṭaril, `cōḻaṉ nalaṅkiḷḷi tampi māvaḷat tāṉum' eṉṟiruppatāṟ peṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṉ (nalaṅkiḷḷi) cēṭceṉṉi, puṭpaki, tērvaṇ kiḷḷi eṉṉum peyarkaḷāṉum kūṟappaṭuvaṉ. ivaṉ āvūrilum uṟiyūrilum muṟṟukiyiṭṭu neṭuṅkiḷḷiyi veṉṟataṉōṭu, avaṉikkāriyāṟu eṉṉu miṭattiṟ porutu koṉṟāṉ. avviṭattiṟṟāṉē cēra pāṇṭiyarkaḷiyum veṉṟu avarkaḷ koṭiyik kip paṟṟiṉāṉ. ivaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭiluḷḷa ēḻu araṇkaḷi veṉṟu kippaṟṟi, avaṟṟiṉkaṇ taṉatu pulikkoṭiyi nāṭṭiṉāṉ eṉṟu 33ām puṟappāṭṭiṉāl terikiṟatu. ivaṉ aḷaviṭaṟkariya taṉatu tāṉiyuṭaṉ valamākap puṟappaṭṭu muṉ pāṇṭiyaṉiyum, piṉ cēraṉiyum veṉṟu avvaḷavil amiyāṉāy vaṭanāṭṭaracarkaḷiyum veṉṟu valiyaḻittu mīḷvaṉ eṉap pulavarkaḷ ivaṉip pārāṭṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ.
"	taliyōr nuṅkiṉ ṟīñcēṟu miciya
iṭiyōr paḻattiṉ piṅkaṉi māntak
kaṭiyōr, viṭuvāyp piciroṭu cuṭukiḻaṅku nukara
nilamalar viyattu valamuṟi vaḷii
vēntupī ṭaḻitta ēntuvēṟ ṟāṉiyoṭu"
eṉa ālattūrkiḻār ivaṉatu paṭipperumiyiyum, veṟṟit tiṟattiyum aḻakākap puṉinturittaṉar. 'muṉ cellum tūcippaṭiyōr paṉiyiṉ nuṅkiṉi uṇṇa, iṭiyē celvōr ataṉ paḻattiṉi nukara, piṉ celvōr paṉiyiṉ cuṭṭa kiḻaṅkiṉi uṇṇa iṅṅaṉam ulakattu valamuṟiyākac cūḻntu māṟṟaracari valikeṭutta cēṉiyōṭu' eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. ikkaruttē kōvūrkiḻārāl vēṟu vakiyākak kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu. avar, 'kīḻkaṭal piṉṉatāka mēl kaṭaliṉ veḷḷiya ali niṉ kutiriyiṉ kuḷampi yalippa valamāka muṟiyē varutalu muṇṭāmeṉṟu vaṭanāṭṭuḷḷa aracukaḷ kalaṅki neñcilē avalam mikat tuyilāta kaṇkaḷi yuṭiyavāyiṉa' eṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉam oppaṟṟa vīraṉākiya immaṉṉaṉ pulavarkaḷi naṉku matittu, avarkaḷ kūṟum aṟivurikaḷik kēṭṭu avvāṟoḻuki vantaṉaṉ eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ivaṉ uṟiyūri muṟṟuki yiṭṭirunta poḻutu, kōvūr kiḻār eṉṉuñ cāṉṟōr ōr pāṭṭiyaṟṟik kūṟi, ivaṉiyum ivaṉ pakivaṉākiya neṭuṅkiḷḷiyiyum cantu ceyvittār. avvariya ceyyuḷ,
"	irumpaṉi veṇṭōṭu malintō ṉallaṉ
karuñciṉi vēmpiṉ ṟeriyalō ṉallaṉ
niṉṉa kaṇṇiyum ārmiṭin taṉṟē, niṉṉoṭu
poruvōṉ kaṇṇiyum ārmiṭin taṉṟē
oruvīr tōṟpiṉun tōṟpatuṅ kuṭiyē
iruvīr vēṟa liyaṟkiyu maṉṟē, ataṉāl
kuṭipporu ḷaṉṟu nuñ ceyti koṭittēr
nummō raṉṉa vēntarkku 
meymmali yuvaki ceyyumiv vikalē"
eṉpatu. 'periya paṉiyiṉ tōṭṭiṉic cūṭiṉōṉallaṉ; kariya kompiṉiyuṭiya vēmpiṉatu māliyi uṭiyōṉallaṉ; niṉatu kaṇṇiyum āttiyāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu; niṉṉuṭaṉ pōr ceyvāṉatu kaṇṇiyum āttiyāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu; ātalāl nummuḷ oruvīr tōṟpiṉum tōṟpatu num kuṭiyaṉṟō? iruvīrum vellutal iyalpumaṉṟu; ātalāl numatu ceyki num kuṭikkut takkataṉṟu; immāṟupāṭu nummippōlum tērvēntarkku uṭampu pūrikkum uvakiyic ceyyum; itu tavirtalē numakkut takkatu.' eṉpatu ataṉ poruḷākum.
ivvaracaṉi uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār pāṭiya mutumoḻikkāñci eṉṉum tuṟiyaminta ceyyuṭkaḷ paṭittaṟin tiṉpuṟaṟpālaṉa. 'nūṟu itaḻkaḷiyuṭiya tāmari malariṉ niri pōl ēṟṟattāḻvillāta ciṟanta kuṭiyiṟpiṟantu vīṟṟirun tōrkaḷi eṇṇumiṭattup pukaḻum pāṭṭum uṭiyōr cilar; tāmariyiṉ iliyiyoppap payaṉpaṭātu māyntōr palar; pulavarāṟ pāṭappaṭum pukaḻuṭiyōr tām ceyyum nalviṉiyi muṭittu, vicumpilē pākaṉāṟ celuttap paṭāta vimāṉattip poruntuvar eṉṟu aṟivuṭiyōr kūṟuvar; entiyē, nalaṅkiḷḷi, tēytaluṇmiyum perukaluṇmiyum māytaluṇmiyum piṟattaluṇmiyum kalvi mukattāl aṟiyātavarum aṟiyumpaṭi kāṭṭit tiṅkaḷākiya teyvam iyaṅkukiṉṟa ulakattil oṉṟil vallarāyiṉum allarāyiṉum vaṟumiyāl varuntivantōrkku aruḷ purivāyāka' eṉṟum; 'tīyōri oṟuttalum nallōrkku aruḷpaṇṇutalumākiya muṟimiyiṉ maṭintirātu, nallataṉ nalaṉum tīyataṉ ṟīmiyum illayeṉpōrkku iṉamākā toḻika; viḻaviṉkaṇ āṭum kūttaratu vēṟupaṭṭa kōlampōla muṟi muṟiyē tōṉṟi iyaṅki iṟantu pōkiṉṟa ivvulakattil niṉcuṟṟam makiḻcciyiṭattatāka; nī pātukātta poruḷ pukaḻiṭat tatāka' eṉṟum; piṟavāṟum avaṟṟiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. muṉpu pōr ceytu veṟṟi koḷḷutaliyē poruḷākak koṇṭirunta immaṉṉaṉ mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉārāl aṟivuṟuttap peṟṟu aṟañ ceytali mēṟ koṇṭāṉāvaṉ.
iṉi, ivvēntaṉ kaviti yiyaṟṟum vaḷamuṭiya ciṟanta pulavaṉākavum viḷaṅkiṉaṉeṉa ivaṉ pāṭiya iraṇṭu aḻakiya ceyyuṭkaḷ puṟanāṉūṟṟil uḷḷamiyāl aṟiyappaṭukiṟatu. avviraṇṭu ceyyuṭkaḷil oṉṟu māṟṟāri nōkki vañciṉaṅ kūṟuvatāka vuḷḷatu. atilē, 'mella vantu eṉ nallaṭi porunti īyavēṇṭumeṉṟu irapparāyiṉ avarkku eṉ aracāṭci koṭuttalō eḷitu; iṉiya uyirēyāyiṉum koṭuppēṉ; āṟṟaluṭiyōratu valiyip pōṟṟātu eṉ uḷḷattiyikaḻnta aṟivillātōṉ tuyilkiṉṟa puliyi yiṭaṟiya kuruṭaṉ pōla uyntu pōtalō aritu; mūṅkilit tiṉṉum yāṉiyiṉ kālil akappaṭṭa nīṇṭa mūṅkiṉ muḷiyiyoppa avviṭattu varuntumpaṭi mēṟceṉṟu porutilēṉāyiṉ, kuṟṟamaṟṟa neñcattāṟ kātalkoḷḷāta potu makaḷiratu poruntāta muyakkattāl eṉmāli tuvaḷvatāka'eṉṟu kūṟiyiruppatu kiḷḷiyiṉ perumita vuṇarcciyiyum, uḷattūymiyiyum naṉku pulappaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. maṟṟoru ceyyuḷ 'tam kuṭiyiṉ mutiyōrkaḷik kūṟṟam koṇṭupōka, ūḻiṉālē tampāl vanta, muṉṉōriṉ veṟṟiyāluṇṭākiya aracurimiyip peṟṟēmāyiṉ ittalimiyip peṟṟēmeṉak koṇṭu tam kuṭimakkaḷi iṟi vēṇṭiyirakkum āṇmiyillāta uḷḷañciṟiyōṉ peṟiṉ, avvaracurimi avaṉukkut tāṅka voṇṇātāmpaṭi mikavum kaṉattatu; maṉaveḻucciyum muyaṟciyumuṭiya cīriyōṉ peṟuvaṉāyiṉ, veṇkuṭiyum muracumuṭiya aracāṭciyip poruntiya celvam tāḻnta nīriyuṭiya vaṟṟiya kuḷattiṭattuc ciṟiya neṭṭiyiṉ ularnta cuḷḷipōlap peritum noytākum' eṉṉum poruḷuṭiyatu. itiliruntu avaṉuṭiya uḷḷamikutiyum, kuṭikaḷippurakkuṅ koḷkiyum naṉku veḷippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ivvaracaṉip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ uṟiyūr mutukaṇṇaṉ cāttaṉār, kōvūrkiḻār, ālattūrkiḻār eṉpōr. ivaruḷ, ivaṉ tuñciyapiṉ iruntu iraṅkippāṭiyavar ālattūr kiḻār. kōṉāṭṭu eṟiccilūr māṭalaṉ maturikkumaraṉ eṉṉum pulavarāṟ pāṭappaṭṭa ilavantip paḷḷittuñciya nalaṅkiḷḷicēṭceṉṉi yeṉpāṉ immaṉṉaṉēyāyiṉ ivaṉ ilavantippaḷḷi eṉṉumiṭattil uyir tuṟantāṉāvaṉ. maturik kumaraṉārum ivaṉip pāṭiṉārāvar.



12.  tittaṉ
ivvaracaṉ uṟiyūriliruntavaṉ. ivaṉ makaṉ pōrvik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷi eṉpāṉ. 
"	iḻiyaṇi paṇittōḷ aiyi tanti
maḻivaḷan tarūu māvaṇ ṭittaṉ"
eṉa akattilē paraṇarāl ivaṉ pārāṭṭappaṭuvatiliruntu immaṉṉaṉ peruṅ koṭiyāḷi eṉpatum, kaṟpiṟ ciṟanta aiyi eṉpāḷ ivaṉukkup putalvi eṉpatum veḷiyākiṉṟaṉa.
"tittaṉ piṇṭa nelliṉ uṟanti"
"	nocci vēlit tittaṉ uṟantik
kaṉmutir puṟaṅkāṭ ṭaṉṉa"
"	palanalla pulaṉaṇiyum
cīrcāṉṟa viḻucciṟappiṟ
ciṟukaṇ yāṉip peṟalarun tittaṉ
uṟanti"
eṉap puṟanāṉūṟṟilum muṟiyē paraṇarum nakkīrarum uṟantiyi ivaṉuk kurimippaṭutturippatu mutaliyavaṟṟāl ivaṉ uṟiyūric ciṟanta matilaraṇum kāṭṭaraṇum uṭiyatākac ceytu pakiveṉṟu kuṭikaḷi naṉku puṟantantu pukaḻciṟantu viḷaṅkiṉaṉ eṉap pulaṉākiṉṟatu. vaṭuka aracaṉākiya kaṭṭi eṉpavaṉ orukāl ittit taṉuṭaṉ pōrceyyak karuti vaṭanāṭṭil aracupurinta pāṇaṉ eṉṉum vīraṉuṭaṉ cērntu uṟiyūriyaṭintu ōr puṟattil irukkiyil avaṉatu nāḷavikkaṇ olikkappeṟum kiṇiyōci kēṭṭu accamuṟṟup porātu ōṭiṉaṉ. itu,
"	valimiku muṉpiṟ pāṇaṉoṭu malitārt
tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ uṟanti nāḷavip
pāṭiṉ ṟeṇkiṇip pāṭukēṭ ṭañcip
pōraṭu tāṉik kaṭṭi
porāa tōṭiya vārppiṉum peritē"
eṉa akappāṭṭiṟ kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. ittaku ciṟappu vāynta ivvēntaṉ taṉ mintaṉākiya pōrvikkōpperunaṟkiḷḷi yuṭaṉ ekkāraṇattālō pakimi koṇṭiruntāṉ. avaṉ taṉpōlum tāḷāṇmiyum vīramum ilaṉāy maṭin tiruntamiyāl veṟuppuṟṟu avaṉit taṉatu nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum ōṭṭiviṭṭaṉaṉpōlum? eṉiṉum, tantiyāl nāṭiḻantōṭi vaṟumiyāṟ pullaricik kūḻ uṇṭirunta perunaṟkiḷḷi piṉpu mikka valimiyum pōrttiṟalum vāyntavaṉāyiṉāṉ; mukkā vaṉāṭṭu āmūrmallaṉ eṉpāṉoṭu maṟpōr porutu avaṉik koṉṟu pukaḻpeṟṟāṉ. icceytikaḷ cāttantiyārum peruṅkōḻināykaṉ makaḷ nakkaṇṇiyārum pōrvik kōpperunaṟkiḷḷiyiṉmītu arumiyākap pāṭiyuḷḷa puṟap pāṭṭukkaḷāl veḷippaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
iṉi, tittaṉ eṉṉum iccempiyar kuricil ciṟanta pulavaṉākavum tikaḻntaṉaṉ eṉpatu ivaṉ pāṭiya pāṭṭoṉṟu akanāṉūṟṟil iruppataṉāl aṟiyappaṭum. ivaṉ tittaṉ veḷiyaṉ eṉavum vīriveḷiyaṉ tittaṉ eṉavum kūṟappaṭuvaṉ. ivaṉatu kālam kāriyāṟṟut tuñciya neṭuṅkiḷḷiyiṉ kālattiṟku muṟpaṭṭatākumeṉṟu karutappaṭukiṟatu. ivaṉip pukaḻntu pāṭiṉōr paraṇarum, nakkīrarumāvar.


13. peruṅkiḷḷi
ivvaracaṉ uṟiyūril iruntavaṉ; ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ eṉṉum cērar perumāṉukku ammāṉ makaṉ. ivaṉ, cōḻaṉ kiḷḷi vaḷavaṉukkum nalaṅkiḷḷikkum pakivaṉāyirunta neṭuṅ kiḷḷiyiṉ mintaṉ eṉṟum, ivaṉē irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉap piṉpu vaḻaṅkap peṟṟāṉāvaṉ eṉṟum karutuvar tiruvāḷar mu. irākaviyaṅkār avarkaḷ.1 125,174-ām puṟappāṭṭukkaḷi neṭuṅkiḷḷiyiṉ varalāṟṟōṭu iyiya vittu nōkkumiṭattu aṅṅaṉam karutuvatu poruttamākavē tōṉṟukiṟatu. ivaṉ peyar perunaṟkiḷḷi yeṉavum vaḻaṅkum. ivaṉ āṇṭil iḷiyaṉāyp paṭṭameytiya poḻutu cōṇāṭṭilirunta ivaṉuṭiya ñātiyaracar palar kūṭi ivaṉukku māṟākak kalakam viḷippārāyiṉar. ataṉāl, ivaṉ, taṉakkut tuṇivaṉākavirunta maliyamāṉatu muḷḷūrmaliyil ōṭi oḷikkumpaṭi nērntatu. appoḻutu maliyamāṉ tirumuṭikkāriyiṉ makaṉum, cōḻaṟkup paṭit talivaṉākaviruntu ciṟappup peṟṟavaṉumākiya cōḻiyavēṉāti tirukkaṇṇaṉ eṉpāṉ ivaṉip pātukāttu ivaṉ kuṭi nilipeṟumpaṭi ceytāṉ. icceyti, tirukkaṇṇaṉi māṟōkattu nappacaliyār pāṭiya puṟappāṭṭāl iṉitu viḷaṅkukiṟatu. atil,
"	araciḻan tirunta vallaṟ kāli
muraceḻun tiraṅku muṟṟamoṭu kariporu
tiraṅkupuṉa ṉeritaru mikuperuṅ kāviri
mallal naṉṉāṭ ṭallal tīra
...	....	....
eḷḷaṟu ciṟappiṉ muḷḷūr mīmici
aruvaḻi yirunta peruviṟal vaḷavaṉ
matimaruḷ veṇkuṭi kāṭṭi akkuṭi 
putumiyiṉ niṟutta pukaḻmēm paṭuna"
eṉṟu tirukkaṇṇaṉ pārāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟāṉ.
iṅṅaṉam iḷiyaṉākiya peruṅkiḷḷikku nērnta iṭukkaṇik kēḷviyuṟṟāṉ vīraruḷ vīraṉākiya cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ; uṭaṉē cōṇāṭṭiṉmītu paṭiyeṭuttuc ceṉṟu, tam mittuṉaṟkup pakivarāyirunta cōḻar oṉpatiṉmari oru pakaṟpoḻutil uṟiyūriṉ teṟku vāyiṟ puṟattatākiya nērivāyil eṉṉum iṭattiṟ porutuvīḻtti, avaṉatu āṇi iṉitu naṭakkumāṟu ceytaṉaṉ. icceyti,
"	mittuṉa vaḷavaṉ kiḷḷiyoṭu poruntā
otta paṇpiṉar oṉpatu maṉṉar
iḷavaracu poṟāar ēval kēḷār
vaḷanā ṭaḻikku māṇpiṉa rātaliṉ
oṉpatu kuṭiyum orupaka loḻittavaṉ 
poṉpuṉi tikiri yoruvaḻip paṭuttōy" 
eṉac cilappatikāra nīrppaṭikkātiyilum,
"	ārpuṉi teriya loṉpatu maṉṉari
nēri vāyil nilicceru veṉṟu"
eṉa naṭukaṟkātiyilum ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉi nōkki māṭalaṉ kūṟuṅ kūṟṟāka iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ urittalāl naṉku pulaṉā kiṉṟatu. patiṟṟuppattiṉ aintām pattup patikattilum,
"	ārāc ceruviṟ cōḻarkuṭik kuriyōr
oṉpatiṉmar vīḻa vāyiṟpuṟat tiṟuttu"
eṉa itu kuṟikkappeṟṟuḷatu. ivaṉ uṟiyūriṉ kaṇṇē kaṇṇakikkuk kōyil eṭuttu viḻāc ceytaṉaṉ eṉpatu "atu kēṭṭuc cōḻaṉ peruṅkiḷḷi kōḻiyakattu ettiṟattāṉum varantarumivaḷōr pattiṉik kaṭavuḷākumeṉa naṅkikkup pattiṉik kōṭṭamuñ camittu nittal viḻāvaṇi nikaḻvit tōṉē" eṉṉum cilappatikāra uripeṟu kaṭṭuriyāṟ peṟappaṭum.
iṉi, ivaṉ maliyaṉit tuṇiyākak koṇṭu cēramāṉ māntarañcēra lirumpoṟiyuṭaṉ porutuveṉṟāṉ eṉpatu "paruttip peṇṭiṉ paṉuva laṉṉa" eṉṉum puṟappāṭṭi ṉālum, cēramāṉ māveṇkōvum, pāṇṭiyaṉ kāṉap pērtanta ukkirap peruvaḻutiyum ivaṉukku naṭpāḷar eṉpatu oḷaviyār pāṭiya "nākat taṉṉapākār maṇṭilam" eṉṉum puṟap pāṭṭiṉālum peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. irācacūyam vēṭṭa perunaṟkiḷḷi eṉa ivaṉukku vaḻaṅkum peyarāṉē ivaṉ avvēḷvi purintaṉaṉ eṉpatum peṟṟām. ivaṉip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ pāṇṭaraṅkaṇṇaṉār, oḷaviyār, ulōccaṉār eṉpōr.


14. nalluttaraṉ
ivaṉatu varalāṟoṉṟum terintilatēṉum, ivaṉ pāṭiya ōr aḻakiya puṟappāṭṭiliruntu ivaṉ virinta uḷḷamuṭiyāruṭaṉ naṭpuc ceytalil mikka viruppamuṭiyaṉ eṉpatum, ceyyuḷiyaṟṟu vatil vallaṉ eṉpatum pulaṉākiṉṟaṉa. acceyyuḷ, 
"	viḷipatac cīṟiṭam nōkki vaḷikatir
valci koṇṭaḷi malka vikkum 
elimuyaṉ ṟaṉiya rāki uṭiyatam
vaḷaṉvali yuṟukkum uḷami lāḷaroṭu
iyinta kēṇmi illā kiyarō 
kaṭuṅkaṭ kēḻal iṭampaṭa vīḻnteṉa
aṉṟavaṇ uṇṇā tāki vaḻināḷ
perumali viṭarakam pulampavēṭ ṭeḻuntu
iruṅkaḷiṟ ṟoruttal nalvalam paṭukkum
pulipacit taṉṉa melivil uḷḷattu
uraṉuṭi yāḷar kēṇmiyōṭu
iyinta vikal uḷavā kiyarō"
eṉpatu. "viḷinta cevviyi yuṭiya ciṟiya iṭatti nōkki vaḷinta katirākiya uṇavik koṇṭupōy vaḷiyil vikkum elipōlum ciṟu muyaṟciyiṉarākit tam celvatti iṟukap piṭikkum uḷḷa mikuti yillāruṭaṉ poruntiya naṭpu eṉakku illiyākuka; taṟukaṇmiyuṭiya paṉṟiyēṟu iṭappakkattē vīḻntatākaliṉ ataṉi uṇṇutal pulliteṉṟu karuti aṉṟu ataṉi yuṇṇātiruntu aṭuttanāḷ malimuḻiyiṉiṉṟum puṟappaṭṭuc ceṉṟu periya āṇ yāṉiyi valappakkattē vīḻacceytu uṇṇum puli pacittāṟ pōlum melivillāta uḷḷamuṭiya uravōr naṭpuṭaṉ poruntiya nāṭkaḷ uḷavākuka' eṉpatu ataṉ poḻippu. kaṟṟaṟintārēttum kalittokiyil mullikkali pāṭiṉāṉ ivaṉeṉṟāl ivvaracaṉatu pulamiyiṉ perumiyi yārāl aḷaviṭṭurikkalākum?
iṉi, kurāppaḷḷittuñciya kiḷḷivaḷavaṉ, kurāp paḷḷit tuñciya peruntirumāvaḷavaṉ, ceruppāḻi yeṟinta iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi, neytalaṅkāṉal iḷañcēṭ ceṉṉi, muṭittalik kōpperu naṟkiḷḷi, vēṟpaḵṟaṭakkip peruviṟaṟkiḷḷi mutaliya cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷ caṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiṟ kūṟappaṭṭiruk kiṉṟaṉarēṉum avarkaḷippaṟṟi mikutiyāka aṟiyalāvaṉa illi. ciṟappuṭiya iru cōḻa vēntariṉ varalāṟukaḷē iṉi ippakutiyil veḷiyiṭaṟ kuriyavākum.


15. kōpperuñ cōḻar
ivar, paṇṭoru kālattil oru kōḻiyāṉatu yāṉi yoṉṟip pōrilveṉṟa iṭamātaliṉ 'kōḻi' eṉap peyar peṟṟatum, toṉṟu toṭṭuc cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷ vīṟṟiruntu aracāḷap peṟṟa tali nakaraṅkaḷuḷḷē mikka paḻami poruntiyatum, aṟaṅkaḷ nilipeṟṟa avikkaḷattit taṉṉakattē koṇṭiruntatum, 'ūreṉappaṭuvatuṟiyūr' eṉa āṉṟōr palarālum ciṟappit tōtap peṟuvatum ākiya uṟantiyam patiyil iruntavar; kalvik kaṭaliṉ elli kaṇṭavar; tīñcuvi keḻumiya centamiḻppākkaḷ pāṭuvatiṟ ciṟappumikkavar; aṟattiṉ nuṭpaṅkaḷiyellām aṟintu tām avvāṟoḻukuvatōṭu, yāvarum aṟampurintu mēṉmiyuṟavēṇṭu meṉṉum peru viruppiṉar; pulavar perumakkaḷiṭattut tām vittuḷḷa aṉpālum matippālum avarkaḷuṭiya aṉpiyellām koḷḷi koṇṭavar; avvāṟṟāl tōlā nallici mēlōrākiya picirāntiyār, pottiyār eṉṉum taṇṭamiḻppulavarkaḷ tamakku āruyir naṇparkaḷāka amiyappeṟṟavar.
picirāntiyār eṉpavar pāṇṭināṭṭil picir eṉṉum ūrilirunta oru pulavar. avaratu peyariliruntu avari ātaṉ eṉpāṉukkut tantiyār eṉṟāvatu, oru ātaṉ ṟantiyiṉ vaḻiyil vantu appeyariṭap peṟṟavar eṉṟāvatu koḷḷutal takum. ātaṉṟanti āntiyeṉa maruvumāṟu tolkāppiyattiṟ kūṟappaṭṭuḷatu. picirāntiyār tamatu vāḻnāḷi ṉiṟutik kūṟṟil tamakku nariyillātataṉ kāraṇatti viṉāviya cāṉṟōrkkut tām viṭiyiṟuttalākap pāṭiyuḷḷa pāṭṭiliruntu avaratu kaḷaṅkamaṟṟa uyariya vāḻkki nili pulappaṭum. atu,
"	yāṇṭu palavāka nariyila vākutal
yāṅkā kiyareṉa viṉavuti rāyiṉ
māṇṭaveṉ maṉiviyoṭu makkaḷu nirampiṉar
yāṉkaṇ ṭaṉiyareṉ ṉiḷiyarum vēntaṉum
allavi ceyyāṉ kākku mataṉṟali
āṉṟavin taṭaṅkiya koḷkic
cāṉṟōr palaryāṉ vāḻu mūrē"
eṉpatu. tam maṉivi makkaḷ aṟivilum kuṇattilum māṭci yuṭiyarātalum, ēvalāḷar tām karutiyatik karutiyavāṟē ceytu muṭittalum, tam nāṭṭaracaṉ muṟivaḻuvātu kuṭikaḷip pātukāttalum, tammūriṉkaṇ cāṉṟōr palariruttalum tamakku āṇṭukaḷ pala ceṉṟum nariyillātiruttaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉak kūṟiya avaratu uyariya maṉanili namatu pukaḻccikkaṇ eṅṅaṉam aṭaṅkuvatākum? ippeṟṟiyarāya pāṇṭi nāṭṭup picirāntiyārkkum cōḻanāṭṭuk kōpperuñcōḻarkkum cepputaṟkariya naṭpoṉ ṟuḷatāki vaḷarntu varuvatāyiṟṟu. āṉāl avarkaḷ oru poḻutum oruvari yoruvar nēriṟ kaṇṭavarallar. oruvar oruvaruṭiya kuṇaṅkaḷik kēḷviyuṟṟa māttiriyāṉē avarkaḷ pāl aṭaṅkiyirunta uḻuvalaṉpu veḷippaṭṭu avviruvariyum otta vuṇarcciyuṭiya naṭpiṉarākki viṭṭatu. avarkaḷ oruvaroruvarippaṟṟip pēcāta nāḷum, niṉiyāta nāḻikiyum iruntiruttal cālātu. picirāntiyār orunāḷ mālippoḻutil tamatāruyir naṇpaṉi niṉintu kātal kimikku aṉṉaccēval oṉṟi. viḷittup pāṭukiṉṟa pāṭṭu annaṭpiṉiyum peṭpiṉiyum tiṭpamuṟakkāṭṭum. atu,
"	aṉṉac cēval! aṉṉac cēval!
āṭukoḷ veṉṟi yaṭupō raṇṇal
nāṭutali yaḷikku moṇmukam pōlak
kōṭukūṭu matiya mukiḻnilā viḷaṅkum
miyaṉ māliyām kiyaṟu piṉiyak
kumariyam peruntuṟi yayiri mānti
vaṭamalip peyarkuvi yāyiṉ iṭiyatu
cōḻanaṉ ṉāṭṭup paṭiṉē kōḻi
uyarnili māṭattuk kuṟumpaṟi yacii
vāyil viṭātu kōyil pukkem
peruṅkōk kiḷḷi kēṭka irumpici
rānti yaṭiyuṟi yeṉiṉē māṇṭaniṉ
iṉpuṟu pēṭi yaṇiyattaṉ
aṉpuṟu naṉkalam nalkuvaṉ niṉakkē"
eṉpatu. 'aṉṉac cēvalē! aṉṉac cēvalē!! em aṇṇal taṉ nāṭṭiṉit taliyaḷi ceyyum mukampōla matiyam viḷaṅkum mālippoḻutiṉkaṇ yām ceyalaṟṟu varuntā niṟkiṉṟōm; nī tāṉ kumariyāṟṟiṉ peruntuṟiyil ayiriyi mēyntu vaṭaticik kaṇṇatākiya imayamalikkuc celkuti yāyiṉ, iraṇṭiṟkum iṭiyiluḷḷatākiya cōḻanāṭṭi aṭintaviṭattu, uṟiyūriṉkaṇ uyarntu tōṉṟum māṭat tiṭattē niṉ peṭiyōṭu taṅki, vāyil kāvalarkku uṇartti viṭātē taṭiyiṉṟik kōyiliṟ pukuntu, em peruṅkōvākiya kiḷḷi kēṭka 'yāṉ picirāntiyiṉ aṭiyuṟi' eṉṟu colviyāyiṉ niṉ iṉpuṟum pēṭi aṇiyat taṉ viruppamuḷḷa aṇikalatti niṉakkut taruvaṉ' eṉpatu itaṉ poḻippu.
ivvāṟākiya uttama naṭpiṉāl viḷiyum iṉpattip pārkkilum ivvulakattuc ciṟanta viṉpam  yātuḷatu? taliyāyār tammuṭceyta naṭpuk karumpi nuṉiyiliruntu tiṉṟāṟ pōlavum, pēraṟivāḷarkaḷiyaṟṟiya nūlkaḷiṉ nayam avaṟṟiṟ payilun tōṟum veḷippaṭṭu iṉpam viḷittal pōlavum mēṉ mēl vaḷarntu iṉpam payappatāmeṉak kūṟuvar āṉṟōr. tuṟakka viṉpamum itaṟku nikarākāteṉil itaṉ perumiyi vēṟu kūṟutal eṉ? kōpperuñ cōḻarum picirāntiyārum kāṇṭaliṉṟiyum iṅṅaṉamāka īṇṭiya naṭpiṉ payaṉ tuyttu varuṅkālattil nikaḻvatoru ceyti neñciṉiyurukkun taṉmiyatu.
ivvaracar peruntakikku iraṇṭu makkaḷ uḷarāyiṉar. 
"makaṉurikkum tanti nalatti"
eṉṟum, 
"	takkār takavila reṉpa tavaravar
eccattāṟ kāṇap paṭum".
eṉṟum āṉṟōr kūṟum potunītikku vilakkāka orō vaḻit takavilārkku naṉ makkaḷum, takavuṭiyārkkut tīya makkaḷum uṇṭākiṉṟaṉar. kōpperuñcōḻariṉ makkaḷum pāṟkaṭaliṟ ṟōṉṟiya ālālampōlum koṭiya paṇpiṉarāyiruntaṉar. aṉpiṟkē nilikkaḷamākiya tam arumit tantiyuṭaṉ pōr ceyyavum eḻuntaṉareṉṟāl aṉṉavariṉ tīyapaṇpi eṉṉeṉ ṟeṭutturippatu? koṭiyavarkaḷāṉa makkaḷ pōr kuṟittu varutalik kōpperuñcōḻar kaṇṭār; ciṉaṅkoṇṭār; tāmum pōr ceytaṟku akkoṭiyārmēṟ ceṉṟār. appoḻutu aṅkiruntu annikaḻcciyip pārttukkoṇṭirunta pullāṟṟūr eyiṟṟiyaṉār eṉṉum pulavarkku ōr nalleṇṇam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. aḵtu avarukku iyaṟkiyumākum. aruñcīrttip peruñcōḻarip pōr ceyyātu taṭukka vēṇṭumeṉpatē avaratu eṇṇam. peruṅkōvip pārttup piṉvarumāṟu kūṟuvārāyiṉar:
'amariṉkaṭ pakivari aṭutalceyta veṉṟiyiyum, valiya muyaṟciyiyum, nila muḻutum niḻalceyyum veṇ koṟṟak kuṭiyiṉiyum uṭiya vēntē, niṉṉuṭaṉ pōr ceytaṟku vanta iruvariyum pārkkumiṭattu avarkaḷ niṉ paki vēntarkaḷ allar; nīyum avarkkup pakivaṉ alli; tōṉṟalē, nī ivvulakattup paranta pukaḻi yeytit tēvarulakattiyaṭinta poḻutu niṉṉāl oḻikkappaṭṭa aracāṭciyurimi avark kuriyatu; ataṉiyum nīyē aṟivi; pukaḻi virumpuvōṉē, niṉṉuṭaṉ pōrceytaṟkeḻunta cūḻcciyaṟṟa aṟiviṉi yuṭiya niṉ putalvar tōṟpiṉ niṉ periya celvatti avarukkaṉṟi yāvarkkuk koṭuppāy? nī avarukkut tōṟpiṉ niṉṉiyikaḻum pakivar uvakkumpaṭiyāka ivvulakattē paḻiyi niṟuttuvāy; ātalāl niṉatu maṟaṉ oḻivatāka; viṇṇōrkaḷ viruppuṭaṉ niṉṉi viruntāka etirkoḷḷumāṟu nalviṉi ceytalvēṇṭum; ataṟku virintu eḻuka; niṉ uḷḷam vāḻvatāka.' 
ivvāṟāka nallicippulavarāl teruṭṭappeṟṟa piṉ, peruṅkōviṉ uḷḷattiṟ poṅkiyeḻunta ciṉam 'iṭunīṟṟāṟ piyavinta nākampōl' aṭaṅkiviṭṭatu. eṉiṉum māṉam eṉpatu aracarkku uyiriṉuñ ciṟantatātaliṉ avar piṉ uyir vāḻa oruppaṭṭilar.
"	māṉa maḻintapiṉ vāḻāmi muṉṉiṉitē"
"	mayirnīppiṉ vāḻāk kavarimā vaṉṉār
uyirnīppar māṉam variṉ"
eṉpaṉa āṉṟōriṉ mutumoḻikaḷallavā? tam makkaḷāl tamatu kulattiṟkum, tamakkum paḻiyuṇṭātaliyuṉṉi uyir tuṟakkat tuṇintār peruñcōḻar. taṟkoli ceytu
koḷvatil paḻiyillātatoru vaḻiyi muṟkālattavar kik
koṇṭiruntaṉar. atutāṉ vaṭakkiruttal eṉpatu. vaṭakkiruttal eṉṟāl yātēṉum iṉṟiyamiyāk kāraṇampaṟṟi uyir tuṟakkat tuṇintavar āṟṟiṭikkuṟipōlum tūyatoru taṉiyiṭattiyeyti, vaṭakkunōkkiyiruntu, uṇavu mutaliyaṉa tuṟantu, kaṭavuṭ cintiyuṭaṉ uyirviṭutalām. peruṅkōvum aṅṅaṉamē vaṭakkiruttali mēṟkoḷvārāyiṉar. vaṭakkiruk kuṅkāl ulakattārkku aṟattiṉ mēṉmiyit tām teḷinta vāṟu aṟivuṟuttic cellavēṇṭumeṉṟu avarkku viruppamuḷa tāyiṟṟu. tām uyir tuṟakkumpoḻutum ulakattārkku utaviceyvatē uyarntōriṉ nōkkameṉpataṟku iḵtoru cāṉṟām. tām aṟivuṟuttak karutiyataṉi nām eṉṟum pōṟṟuvatoru centamiḻc ceyyuḷākap pāṭittantaṉar nam puvikkaracākiya kavikkaracar. atu, 
"	ceykuvaṅ kollō nalviṉi yeṉavē
aiya maṟāar kacaṭīṇṭu kāṭci
nīṅkā neñcattut tuṇivil lōrē
yāṉi vēṭṭuvaṉ āṉiyum peṟumē
kuṟumpūḻ vēṭṭuvaṉ vaṟuṅkiyum varumē
ataṉāl, uyarnta vēṭṭat tuyarntici ṉōrkkuc
ceyviṉi maruṅki ṉeyta luṇṭeṉil
toyyā vulakattu nukarcciyuṅ kūṭum
toyyā vulakattu nukarcci yilleṉiṉ
māṟip piṟappi ṉiṉmiyuṅ kūṭum
māṟip piṟavā rāyiṉum imayattuk
kōṭuyarn taṉṉa tammici naṭṭut
tītil yākkiyoṭu māytal tavattaliyē" (puṟam)
eṉpatu. itaṉporuḷ; 'aḻukku niṟinta teḷivillāta uḷḷattiṉiyuṭiyōr aṟattiṉic ceyvōmō ceyyā tiruppōmō eṉṟu karuti aiyam nīṅkātavarākiṉṟaṉar; yāṉi vēṭṭikkuc celvōṉ yāṉiyiyum eḷitākap peṟuvaṉ; kāṭi vēṭṭikkuc celvōṉ atu peṟāmal veṟuṅki yuṭaṉum tirumpuvaṉ; ataṉāl, uyarnta viruppattiyuṭiya uyarntōrkkut tām ceyta nalviṉip pakutiyāl ataṉi nukartal uṇṭeṉiṉ, avar iruviṉiyum ceyyāta umparulakattiṉ kaṇ iṉpamaṉupavittalum kūṭum; avvulakattu nukarcciyilliyāyiṉ, māṟippiṟakkum piṟappu illiyākavum kūṭum; māṟippiṟattaleṉpatēilliyeṉṟu colvāruḷa rāyiṉ imayamaliyiṉ cikaram ōṅkiṉāṟpōṉṟa tamatu pukaḻi nilipeṟutti vaciyillāta uṭampōṭu kūṭi niṉṟu iṟattal mikavum perumiyuṭiyatu; ataṉāl, evvāṟṟāṉum nalviṉi ceytal aḻakitu' eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam aṟivuṟuttaruḷi, aṅkē kuḻumiyirunta cāṉṟōr kaḷip pārttu 'eṉ naṇpaṉ picirānti ippoḻutu varuvaṉ' eṉṟu peruñcōḻar kūṟiṉar. cāṉṟōrkaḷō avar vārār eṉṟaṉar. atukēṭṭatum peruṅkōvāṉavar 'eṉ uyirip pātukākkum naṇpaṉ pāṇṭi nāṭṭuḷḷum cēymiyiluḷḷatākiya picir eṉṉum ūriṭattāṉ eṉṟu colluvar; eṉiṉum, nām celvam uṭiya kālattu avaṉ vārātiruppiṉum yām tuṉpuṟuṅkālattu vārā tiruppāṉallaṉ' eṉṟaṉar. cāṉṟōrkaṭkō aiyantīrntapāṭilli. kōpperuñ cōḻar piṉṉarum kūṟukiṉṟār : "niṟinta aṟiviṉi yuṭiyīr, 'avaṉ niṉṉik kēḷviyuṟṟiruppatallatu ciṟitupoḻutum kaṇṭatilli; pala āṇṭukaḷ tavaṟātu paḻakippōnta urimi yuṭiyārāyiṉum nī kūṟiyavāṟu vaḻuvutaliṉṟi yoḻukutal aritēyākum' eṉṟu karuti aiyuṟātoḻivīrāka; avaṉ eṉṟum eṉṉip puṟakkaṇittavaṉallaṉ; iṉiya kuṇaṅkaḷi yuṭiyaṉ; piṇitta naṭpiṉi yuṭiyaṉ; pukaḻi yaḻikkum poymmiyi virumpāṉ; taṉ peyarip piṟarkkuc collumpoḻutu `eṉ peyar kōpperuñcōḻaṉ' eṉṟu eṉ peyariyē taṉ peyarākac collum aṉpiṉ urimiyiyum uṭiyaṉ; ataṉmēlum ippaṭi yāṉ tuyara muṟuṅkālattu āṇṭu nillāṉ; ippoḻutē varuvaṉ; avaṉukku iṭamoḻittu vippīrāka"  eṉṟaṉar.
iṅṅaṉaṅkūṟik kōpperuñcōḻar vaṭakkirukkavē, picirāntiyārum avar kūṟiyāṅku appoḻutu avaṇ vantu cērntaṉar. picirāntiyār pōṉṟē peruṅkōvukku āruyir naṇparum, uṟiyūriliruntavarum ākiya pottiyār eṉṉum pulavar ataṉik kaṇṭār; aḷaviṭamuṭiyāta viyappiṉiyum, aṭakkavoṇṇāta tuyarattiṉiyum uṭiyarāyiṉār. appoḻutu avar kūṟiyatu,
"	niṉakkuṅ kāli maruṭki yuṭittē eṉipperuñ ciṟappiṉō ṭīṅkitu tuṇital
ataṉiṉu maruṭki yuṭittē piṟaṉāṭṭut
tōṟṟañ cāṉṟa cāṉṟōṉ pōṟṟi
icimara pāka naṭpuk kantāka
iṉiyatōr kāli yīṅku varutal
varuvaṉeṉṟa kōṉatu perumiyum
atupaḻu tiṉṟi vantava ṉaṟivum
viyattoṟum viyattoṟum viyappiṟan taṉṟē
ataṉāl, taṉkō liyaṅkāt tēyat tuṟiyuñ
cāṉṟō ṉeñcuṟap peṟṟa toṉṟici
aṉṉōṉi yiḻanta vivvulakam
eṉṉā vatukol aḷiyatu tāṉē"	(puṟam)
eṉpatu.
itaṉ poruḷ : 'ivvaracaṉ ettuṇiyum periya ciṟappuk kaḷuṭaṉ kūṭiyum avaṟṟiyellām kiviṭṭu ivviṭattē ippaṭi varat tuṇital niṉikkumiṭattu viyappiṉiyuṭiyatu; vēṟṟu vēntaṉatu nāṭṭiṉkaṇ viḷakkamaminta cāṉṟōṉ pukaḻ mēmpāṭāka naṭpē paṟṟukkōṭāka iṉṉatōr iṉṉākkālattu vaḻuviṉṟi ivviṭattu varutal ataṉiṉum viyappiyuṭiyatu; ivvāṟu varuvaṉeṉṟu tuṇintu colliya vēntaṉatu perumiyum, avaṉ colliya coṟ paḻutiṉṟāka vantavaṉatu aṟivum viyakkuntōṟum viyakkuntōṟum viyappu ellikaṭantuḷatu; ātalāl, taṉ ceṅkōl cellāta tēyat tuṟiyum cāṉṟōṉatu neñcit taṉṉiṭattē urittākap peṟṟa paḻiya pukaḻiyuṭiya attaṉmiyuḷḷa periyōṉi yiḻanta ittēyam eṉṉa tuṉpamuṟuṅ kollō! itutāṉ iraṅkattakkatu!' eṉpatu.
iṅṅaṉam pottiyār viyantum kiyaṟṟum kūṟumāṟu ceṉṟa picirāntiyārum vaṭakkiruntaṉar.1 maṟṟum vaṭakkiruntār palarāvar. eṉṉē aṉpiṉ takimi! aṉpeṉpatu pala uṭampiluḷḷa uyirkaḷi oṉṟākap piṇikkun taṉmiyāṟṟāṉ ataṟku nār eṉṟu peyar uṇṭāyiṟṟātal vēṇṭum. iṅṅaṉam peruṅkōvuṭaṉ oruṅkē vaṭakkiruntār palarākavum, avarkku uyir naṇparākiya pottiyār māttiram uṭaṉ vaṭakkirun tārallar. ataṟkuk kāraṇam avar maṉivi karuvuṟṟirun tamiyāl kōpperuñcōḻar avarit taṭuttu 'niṉakku makaṉ piṟantapiṉ varuka' eṉṟu kūṟiya moḻiyi maṟukkalāṟṟāmiyē yākum. eṉavē avar uṭaṉuyir tuṟavāmikkum naṇpaṉ colli maṟukkalāṟṟāta avaratu naṭpurimiyē kāraṇamāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu teḷivu. picirāntiyārum, peruṅkōvum vaṭakkiruntami kaṇṭu muṟiyē kaṇṇakaṉār, karuvūrp peruñcatukkattup pūtanātaṉār eṉṉum pulavarkaḷ iraṅkip pāṭiyuḷḷārkaḷ. kaṇṇakaṉār pāṭiyatu,
"	poṉṉun tukiru muttu maṉṉiya
māmali payanta kāmaru maṇiyum 
iṭipaṭac cēya vāyiṉun toṭipuṇarntu
aruvili naṉkala mamikkuṅ kāli
oruvaḻit tōṉṟiyāṅ keṉṟuñ cāṉṟōr
cāṉṟōr pāla rāpa
cālār cālār pālarā kupavē"		(puṟam)
eṉṉum aḻakiya pāṭṭākum. 'poṉṉum pavaḷamum, muttum, maṇiyum nilam, kaṭal, mali eṉpavaṟṟil oṉṟuk koṉṟu neṭiya cēymiyiliruppiṉum, ariya viliyiṉiyuṭiya nalla aṇikalaṉkaḷic ceyyum poḻutu avi ōriṭattut tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōla eppoḻutum cāṉṟōr cāṉṟōr pakkattiṉarāvar' eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷām.
pottiyār eṉṉum pulavar tam naṇpar colli maṟātu ceṉṟu uṟiyūrik kaṇṭapoḻutum piṉ porukāl aracaṉatu naṭukallik kaṇṭapoḻutum iraṅkippāṭiya pāṭṭukkaḷ neñciṉiyurukkun taṉmiyaṉa. 'ceṅkōliṉaṉ, tiṇṇiya aṉpiṉaṉ, makaḷirkku melliyaṉ, vīrarkku vīraṉ, uyarn tōrkkup pukaliṭam; avaṉi attaṉmiyaṉeṉṟu karutātu kūṟṟam uyir koṇṭupōyiṟṟu; ātalāl vāymiyuṭiya pulavarkaḷē, nam tuṉpuṟṟa cuṟṟattiyum cērttukkoṇṭu ak kūṟṟatti vivōm vārīr'eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟār. ivvāṟu tam āruyir naṇpaṉip pirintu orunāḷum poṟuttiruk kalāṟṟāta pottiyārum, tamakku makaṉ piṟanta piṉṉarum vāḷā amin tirupparō? makaṉum piṟantāṉ; avaruñ ceṉṟār; ceṉṟu naṭukallikkaṇṭu avar, `niḻalik kāṭṭilum niṉṉip piriyātuṟiyum niṉ viruppamuḷḷa maṉivi vayiṟṟu makaṉ piṟantapiṉ vā' eṉaccolli eṉṉi iṅku niṉṟum pōkkiya aṉpilātavaṉē, niṉṉōṭu eṉṉiṭiyuḷḷa naṭpiṉi nī eṇṇā tiruppāyalli; pukaḻi virumpuvōṉē, eṉakku nī kuṟittuḷḷa iṭam yātu? colvāyāka' eṉṟaṉar. kōpperuñcōḻarō kallākiyum avarkku iṭaṅ koṭuttaṉar. avarum 'iṉṉuyir virumpuṅ kiḻamit toṉṉaṭpuṭiyōr tammuḻicceliṉ, avar naṭukal ākiyak kaṇṇum iṭaṅkoṭut
taḷippar' eṉṟu ataṉi viyantu kūṟi, vaṭakkiruppārāyiṉar. ivvaracarip pāṭiya pulavarkaḷ picirāntiyār, eyiṟṟiyaṉār, pottiyār, pūtanātaṉār eṉpōr.


16. kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar
cōḻar kulameṉṉum pālāḻiyiṟ ṟōṉṟi aṟaṉum maṟaṉum āṟṟaluṅkuṉṟātu taṉicceṅkōl naṭāttittam pukaḻ niṟuviya maṉṉarkaḷil, civa naṉmakkaḷatu uḷḷatti muḻumaṇip pīṭamākakkoṇṭu vīṟṟiruttaṟkuriyōruḷ mutaṉmiyuṟṟirup pōr kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar eṉpār. apperiyāratu caritam civarkaṭku eyppiṉil vippākak kiṭittirukkum ñāṉa paṇṭāramākiya periya purāṇameṉṉum tiruttoṇṭar purāṇat tirumuṟiyakattē miḷirkiṉṟatu. ataṉ curukkam piṉ varumāṟu: 
'puṟa voṉṟiṉ poruṭṭākat tuli yēṟiyum muṟi purinta cōḻa maṉṉarkkut toṉṟutoṭṭu urimi vāynta tākiya cōṇāṭṭiṉkaṇṇē aliyāl maṇikoḻikkum aḻakiya kāviriyāṟṟiṉ kariyil cantira tīrttattiṉ pakkattē pala tarukkaḷum niṟinta kuḷir pūñcōli oṉṟuḷatu. appūṅkā viṉkaṇ veṇṇāval marattiṉ kīḻē tēva tēvarākiya civaperumāṉ civaliṅka meṉṉum tiru aruḷurut tāṅki eḻuntaruḷiyiruntaṉar. paḻam piṟappil mēlāya tavattiṉic ceyta toru veḷḷāṉi avvaruḷuruvik kaṇṭu aṉpu mēliṭṭuk kiyāl nīriṉi mukantuvantu āṭṭiyum, pūṅkottuk kaḷip paṟittuc cāttiyum nīlakaṇṭarākiya perumāṉi nittalum vaḻipāṭu ceytoḻukāniṉṟatu. ataṉāl tiruāṉikkā eṉṉum peyarum attalattiṟku uḷatāki nilavukiṉṟatu. iḵtivvāṟāka, ñāṉamuṭiyatoru cilantiyāṉatu namparatu cempoṟṟirumuṭimēl caruku mutaliyaṉa utirāvaṇṇam mēl vitāṉam eṉṉumāṟu vāy nūlāl valiyoṉṟu ceṟiyap purintatu. arumiyāka iḻitta accilampivaliyiṉik kaṇṭatu āṇṭavari vaḻipaṭac ceṉṟa avvāṉi; kaṇṭatum ītiṅkiruppatu anucitamāmeṉṟu ataṉic citittatu. cilantiyō muṉpōl mīṇṭum pantar iḻittatu. piṟṟināḷ ataṉiyum āṉi yaḻittatu. pārttatu cilanti; ciṉam mūṇṭatu; 'emperumāṉ tirumēṉiyiṟ caruku viḻāmal yāṉ varunti yiḻitta nūliṉ pantari ivvāṉi aḻippatō' eṉa vempik kotitteḻuntu yāṉayiṉ tutikkiyuṭ pukuntu kaṭittatu. kaṭikkavē yāṉiyāṉatu ataṉip poṟukkalāṟṟātu nilattilē kiyi mōtik kulintu vīḻntatu. kiyiṟ pukunta cilantiyum uyir tuṟantatu. arumaṟip poruḷākum aiyaṉum am matayāṉikkum takumāṟu varamaḷittu, cilantiyāṉatu cōḻar kulattutittu ulaku purantaḷikka aruḷ purintaṉar. akkālattē cōḻa kulattaracaṉām cupatēvaṉ eṉpavaṉ taṉ peruntēviyākiya kamalavatiyuṭaṉ tillit tiruppatiyi aṭintu tirucciṟṟampalattē tiru niruttam puriyum cēvaṭiyi vaṇaṅkit tiruppaṭiyiṉ kīḻ vaḻipāṭu ceytuvantaṉaṉ. tēvi kamalavatiyum iṟivari vaḻipaṭṭu makappēṟu karuti varam vēṇṭiṉaḷ. perumāṉum tiruvuḷḷañ ceytaṉar. muṉ tiruppaṇi purinta cilanti aracu makiḻak kamalavati tiruvayiṟṟil aḻakiya makavāy vanteytiyatu. kamalavati karuvuṟṟup pattut tiṅkaḷum nirampi makap peṟum poḻutu aṭutta kāliyil kālattiṉ iyalpi aṟiyum mutiyā roruvar 'iṉṉum oru nāḻiki kaḻittup piṟakkumēl ikkuḻanti mūṉṟulakamum purakka vallatākum' eṉa iyampiṉar. ataṉik kēṭṭa vaḷavil aṉṉi kamalavatiyum 'oru nāḻiki kaḻittu eṉ piḷḷi piṟakkum paṭiyāka eṉ kālkaḷi mēlētūkkik kaṭṭuṅkaḷ' eṉṟu colli, aṅṅaṉamē aṟiñaruritta naṟpoḻutu vantuṟṟa vaḷavil kāṟ piṇippu viṭappeṟṟu, arumaṇi pōlum putalvaṉip peṟṟeṭuttu 'eṉ kōc ceṅkaṇṇāṉō eṉṟāḷ'. tēvi putalvaṉippeṟṟu uyir tuṟakkavē, ceṅkōl maṉṉaṉākiya cupatēvaṉ āviyaṉiya arum putalvaṉi vaḷarttu muṭicūṭṭuvittut tāṉum tavaneṟi cārntu civalōka meytiṉaṉ.
kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻarum civaperumāṉatu tiruvaruḷāl paḻam piṟappiṉi yuṇarntu aracāṇṭu varukiṉṟār ākaliṉ civaṉār makiḻntuṟiyum tirukkōyil pala camittalākiya tiruttoṇṭiṉik kaṭaṉmiyāka mēṟkoṇṭaṉar. āṉik kāvilē tām muṉpu aruḷ peṟṟataṉi aṟintu ñāṉaccār vākum veṇṇāval marattiṉuṭaṉkūṭa nalañ ciṟantu viḷaṅkumpaṭi perumāṉ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tirukkōyil camittaṉar. maṟṟum, amiccarkaḷi yēvic cōṇāṭṭiṉ paṟpala pakutikaḷilum ammiyappar eḻuntaruḷum cemmiyāya tirukkōyilkaḷ pala camittu akkōyiltōṟum amutupaṭi mutalāṉavaṟṟukku aḷavillāta celvaṅkaḷi ēṟpaṭuttiṉar; tillit tiruppatiyi aṭintu poṉṉampalattē tiruniruttam puriyum perumāṉip pēraṉpuṭaṉ aṭivaṇaṅki uruki uḷaṅkaḷittu uṟiyum nāḷil vāymi kuṉṟāta tillivāḻ maṟiyavarkkut tirumāḷiki pala camittaṉar. ivvāṟāka, pūvulakip potuvaṟap purantaruḷiya kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar civaperumāṉukkuriya tiruttoṇṭukaḷ pala purintu, tēvar pōṟṟat tilliyampalavāṇariṉ tiruvaṭi nīḻali yeytiṉar.
iṉi, ivvēntar perumāṉippaṟṟiya cila ārayccik kuṟippukkaḷ iṅkē eḻututaṟkuriyaṉa :
ivar, cēramāṉ kaṇikkāl irumpoṟi eṉṉum cēramaṉṉaṉuṭaṉ pōrpurintu vākicūṭi, avaṉatu kaḻumalam eṉṉum ūrik kippaṟṟiyatuṭaṉ, cēramāṉiyum piṭittuc ciṟippaṭuttiṉar. appoḻutu kaṇikkālirumpoṟikku uyirttōḻarāyirunta poykiyār eṉṉum pulavar ceṅkaṭ cōḻaratu veṟṟittiṟattic ciṟappittup patiṉeṇkīḻkkaṇakkiṉuḷ oṉṟākiya kaḷavaḻi nāṟpatu eṉṉum nūliṉi iyaṟṟic cēramāṉic ciṟi mīṭṭaṉar. cila pirapantaṅkaḷilē ivvaralāṟu koṇṭu kōcceṅkaṭ cōḻar cuṭṭap paṭukiṉṟār.
"	kaḷavaḻik kaviti poykiyuri ceyya vutiyaṉ kālvaḻit taḷiyi veṭṭiyara ciṭṭa vavaṉum"
eṉṟu kaliṅkattup paraṇiyum,
"	mētakka poyki kavikoṇṭu villavaṉip pātat taḷiviṭṭa pārttivaṉum"
eṉṟu vikkiramacōḻaṉulāvum,
"	aṇaṅku, paṭuttup poṟiyaṉip poykikkuppaṇṭu koṭuttuk kaḷavaḻippāk koṇṭōṉ"
eṉṟu kulōttuṅka cōḻaṉulāvum, iṅṅaṉamē irācarāca cōḻaṉulā mutaliyaṉavum kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
puṟanāṉūṟṟiluḷḷa 'kuḻaviyiṟappiṉum' eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉ kīḻkkuṟippu "cēramāṉ kaṇikkālirumpoṟi cōḻaṉ ceṅkaṇāṉōṭu pōrppuṟattup porutu paṟṟukkōṭ paṭṭuk kuṭavāyiṟ kōṭṭattuc ciṟiyiṟ kiṭantu taṇṇīr tāveṉṟu peṟātu peyarttup peṟṟuk kikkoṇṭiruntu uṇṇāṉ collit tuñciyapāṭṭu" eṉṟuḷatu. tamiḻ nāvalar caritiyil ippāṭṭiṉ kīḻkkuṟippu "cēraṉ kaṇikkālirum poṟi ceṅkaṇāṉāṟ kuṇavāyiṟ kōṭṭattuttaḷip paṭṭapōtu poykiyāruk keḻuti viṭutta pāṭṭu" eṉṟiruk kiṟatu. ivi oṉṟiṉoṉṟu ciṟitu muraṇpaṭiṉum cēramāṉ kaṇikkālirum poṟi kōc ceṅkaṇṇarāṟ ciṟippaṭṭa ceyti ellāvaṟṟiṉum kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu.
 iṉi, tirumaṅkiyāḻvār periya tirumoḻi, tirunaṟiyūrp patikattil
"	kavvimā kaḷiṟunti veṇṇi yēṟṟa
kaḻaṉmaṉṉar maṇimuṭimēṟ kāka mēṟat
teyvavāḷ valaṅkoṇṭa cōḻaṉ cērnta
tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭañ cērmiṉ kaḷē" 
eṉṟum
"	pārāḷa ravarivareṉ ṟaḻunti yēṟṟa
paṭimaṉṉa ruṭaltuṇiyap parimā vuytta
tērāḷaṉ kōccōḻaṉ cērnta kōyil
tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭañ cērmiṉ kaḷē" 
eṉṟum ivvaracarik kuṟippiṭṭiruttalāl, cōḻanāṭṭiluḷḷa veṇṇi, aḻuntūr eṉṉum ūrkaḷil nikaḻnta pōrilum ivar pakiyaracari muṟiyaṭittaṉa reṉpatu terikiṉṟatu. ivvāṟṟāl cēra, pāṇṭiya maṉṉarkaḷ ivaruṭaṉ pōrpurintu tōṟṟuk kīḻppaṭintārātal vēṇṭum. mēṟkuṟitta patikattil tirumaṅki maṉṉaṉ ivarit 'teṉṉāṭaṉ kuṭakoṅkaṉ cōḻaṉ cērnta tirunaṟiyūr maṇimāṭam' eṉpataṉāl mūṉṟu nāṭṭiṟkum uriyarākak kūṟuvatum, cuntaramūrttikaḷ tiruttoṇṭat tokiyil 'teṉṉavaṉā yulakāṇṭa ceṅkaṇārk kaṭiyēṉ' eṉṟu kūṟuvatum iṅkē karutaṟpālaṉa.
tirumaṅki maṉṉaṉ ivarip pukaḻntu kūṟuvatiliruntu, ivar aracarātaṟkēṟpa vēṟu mataṅkaḷiyum purantuvantaṉa reṉpatu peṟappaṭum; eṉiṉum ivarceyta toṇṭukaḷellām civaperumāṉukkēyākum. ivar civaperumāṉukku āṉikkā mutal ampar iṟutiyāka aṟupattunāṉku māṭakkōyilkaḷ kaṭṭiṉareṉṟu cila periyōr kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tirumaṅkiyāḻvārē ivaratu kōyiṟ ṟiruppaṇiyik kuṟittu "irukkilaṅku tirumoḻivā yeṇṭō ḷīcaṟ keḻiṉmāṭam eḻupatu ceytulakamāṇṭa tirukkulattu vaḷaccōḻaṉ" eṉṟu kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. itaṉāl eḻupatu māṭakkōyil eṭuttaṉareṉa aṟiyalākum. māṭak kōyil  eṉpatu peruṅkōyil eṉṟum vaḻaṅkappeṟum. peruṅkōyil ellām kōcceṅkaṇṇar kaṭṭiyavē eṉiṉ, 'perukkāṟu caṭikkaṇinta perumāṉ cērum peruṅkōyi leḻupatiṉō ṭeṭṭum' eṉṟu tirunāvukkaracar aṭivu tirut tāṇṭakattiṟ kūṟiyiruttaliṉ, eḻupatteṭṭup peruṅkōyil ceṅkaṭ cōḻar eṭuttaṉareṉṟu koḷḷavēṇṭum. ivar cilantiyāyiruntu iṟivaṉi vaḻipaṭṭu aracāyiṉa uṇmiyiyum, ivar pala tirukkōyilkaḷ eṭuppitta vaṇmiyiyum tiruneṟit tamiḻvētam aruḷiya civacamaya kuravarkaḷ tam patikaṅkaḷil palaviṭattum pārāṭṭiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷeṉṟāl ivaratu perumiyi nām eṅṅaṉam aḷaviṭṭurikkalākum?
"	ceṅkaṭ peyarkoṇ ṭavaṉcem piyarkōṉ
aṅkaṭ karuṇi peritā yavaṉē
veṅkaṇ viṭiyā yemveṇā valuḷāy
aṅkat tayarvā yiṉaḷā yiḻiyē."
				- tiruñāṉacampantar
"	puttiyiṉāṟ cilantiyuntaṉ vāyi ṉūlāṟ
		potuppanta ratuviḻittuc carukāṉ mēynta
	cittiyiṉā laracāṇṭu ciṟappuc ceyyac
		civakaṇattup pukappeytār tiṟalāṉ mikka 
	vittakattāl veḷḷāṉi viḷḷā vaṉpu 
		viraviyavā kaṇṭataṟku vīṭu kāṭṭip 
	pattarkaḷuk kiṉṉamutām pācūr mēya
		parañcuṭarik kaṇṭaṭiyē ṉuyntavāṟē."
					- tirunāvukkaracar
"	teruṇṭa vāyiṭi nūlkoṇṭu cilanti
		cittirap pantar cikkeṉa viyaṟṟac
	curuṇṭa ceñcaṭi yāratu taṉṉic
		cōḻa ṉākkiya toṭarccikaṇ ṭaṭiyēṉ
	puraṇṭu vīḻntuniṉ poṉmalarp pātam
		pōṟṟi pōṟṟiyeṉ ṟaṉpoṭu pulampi
	aruṇṭeṉ mēlviṉik kañcivan taṭintēṉ
		āvaṭu tuṟi yātiyem māṉē."
				- nampiyārūrar.


17. araciyal
cōḻanāṭṭiṉ elliyum aḷavum ivi yeṉpataṉi.
"	kaṭalkiḻakkut teṟkuk karipuraḷveḷ ḷāṟu
kuṭaticiyiṟ kōṭṭik kariyām - vaṭaticiyil
ēṇāṭṭu veḷḷā ṟirupattu nāṟkātam
cōṇāṭṭuk kelliyeṉac col"
eṉṉum taṉippāṭal uṇarttā niṟkum. cōṇāṭēyaṉṟit toṇṭi nāṭu mutaliyaṉavum paḻiyacōḻamaṉṉarkaḷiṉ āṭciyil aṭaṅkiyiruntaṉa eṉṟāvatu, avarkaḷ āṇikkuk kīḻppaṭṭiruntaṉa eṉṟāvatu kūṟutal poruntum. 1annāḷil, nāṭṭiṉ (maṇṭalattiṉ) pirivukaṭkuk kūṟṟam, kōṭṭam, nāṭu eṉṉum peyarkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. kuṟicci, pāṭi, ūr, kuṭi, pati, pākkam, paṭṭiṉam, nakar mutaliyaṉa ūrkaṭku vaḻaṅkiya peyar kaḷākum. ivaṟṟuḷ paṭṭiṉam, nakar eṉpaṉa pērūr allatu aracaṉ uṟiyum ūrikkuṟikkum. ivviraṇṭiṉuḷ paṭṭiṉam eṉpatu kaṭaṟkariyiluḷḷa nakarākum. nāṭu eṉpatutēm eṉṉum peyarāṉum vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷiṉāl aṟiyalā kiṉṟa cōḻar talinakaraṅkaḷ uṟiyūr, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉṉum iraṇṭumām. cilappatikāra vēṉiṟ kātiyil, 
"	māṭa maturiyum pīṭār uṟantiyum
kalikeḻu vañciyum olipuṉaṟ pukārum"
eṉac cōṇāṭṭiṟku ivviraṇṭu talinakar iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāl oruṅku kuṟippiṭappaṭutal kāṇka. ivaṟṟuḷ uṟiyūr uṟanti eṉavum kōḻi eṉavum vaḻaṅkappaṭum. ōr kōḻiyāṉatu yāṉi yoṉṟip pōrilē tolitta iṭattil amikkap
peṟṟatākaliṉ innakarkkuk kōḻiyeṉap peyaruṇṭāyatu eṉpar;
"	muṟañcevi vāraṇam muṉcama murukkiya
puṟañciṟi vāraṇam pukkaṉar"
eṉa iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ kūṟutaluṅ kāṇka.
maṟṟoru talinakarkkuc campāpati, kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉam eṉṉum peyarkaḷ uṇṭāya varalāṟu maṇimēkaliyiṟ kūṟap paṭṭiruttal muṉ cōṇāṭṭiṉ paḻami kūṟiyaviṭattum, ataṟkuk kākanti eṉṉum peyaruṇṭāyatu kāntaṉ varalāṟuritta viṭattum pōntami kāṇka. pukār eṉṉum peyar ataṟ kuṇṭāyatu marakkalaṅkaḷ pukum tuṟimukamuṭimiyālām. peripḷas 'kamāram' eṉṟum, ṭālami 'kapēris' eṉṟum kūṟiyatu innakariyē eṉpar. it talinakariṉ amippum, ciṟappum karikālaṉ varalāṟuritta viṭattuk kāṭṭappeṟṟaṉa.
cōḻar tamiḻakattu muṭikeḻuvēntar mūvaruḷ oruvarākaliṉ, ivar,
"	paṭiyum koṭiyum kuṭiyum muracum
naṭinavil puraviyum kaḷiṟum tērum
tārum muṭiyum nērvaṉa piṟavum
terivukoḷ ceṅkōl aracarkkuriya"
eṉṟu tolkāppiyam kūṟumāṟu paṭi mutaliya aṉittum uṭiyarāvar. ivaṟṟuḷ muracāṉatu vīramuracu, nītimuracu, īkimuracu eṉa mūvakippaṭum.  vīramuraciṉi ivarkaḷ mika matittu nīrāṭṭi mālicūṭṭip pūcikkum vaḻakkamuṭiyar. cōḻar kulattārkkup potuvāka vaḻaṅkum peyarkaḷ ivi yeṉpataṉi,
"	ceṉṉi vaḷavaṉ kiḷḷi cempiyaṉ
poṉṉit tuṟivaṉ pulikkoṭip puravalaṉ
nēriyaṉ ārkkōṉ nēriṟi apayaṉ
cūriyaṉ puṉaṉāṭaṉ cōḻaṉ peyarē"
eṉṉum tivākarattāl aṟika. ivaṟṟuḷ ceṉṉi, vaḷavaṉ, kiḷḷi, cempiyaṉ, cōḻaṉ eṉpaṉa kuṭipaṟṟiya ciṟappup peyarākavum, aṭiyaṭuttu iyaṟpeyarākavum caṅkac ceyyuṭ
kaḷiṟ payiṉṟuḷḷaṉa. ippeyarkaḷum, apayaṉ eṉpatum oḻinta ēṉiya peyarkaḷ cōḻaruṭiya nāṭu, nakar, mali, yāṟu, koṭi, māli, kulamutalvaṉ eṉpavi paṟṟiyaṉa. puli ivarkaṭkuk koṭiyātalōṭu ilacciṉiyum ām. ār (ātti) kaṇṇiyum tārum ākum. nērimali cērarkkuriyatākap patiṟṟuppattil kūṟappaṭṭiruppatu cintikkaṟpālatu.
cōḻa maṉṉarkaḷ araciyal naṭāttutaṟku amiccar kuḻu, purōkitar kuḻu, tāṉittalivar kuḻu, tūtuvar kuḻu, oṟṟar kuḻu eṉṉum aintu periya kūṭṭaṅkaḷit tuṇiyāka vittuk koṇṭiruntaṉar; ivarkaḷaṉṟi, karaṇattiyalavar, aracaṉatu āṇiyi niṟivēṟṟum karuma vitikaḷ, paṇṭāram vakippō rākiya kaṉakac cuṟṟam, vāyil kāvalar, nakara māntar, paṭittalivar, yāṉi vīrar, kutiri vīrar eṉpōrum avarkaṭkiruntaṉar. naṭpāḷar, aṟivar, maṭittoḻilāḷar, maruttuvakkaliñar, nimittikap pulavar eṉpār uṟuticcuṟṟamāka viḷaṅkiṉar. 
ivi,
"aimperuṅ kuḻuvum eṇpē rāyamum"
eṉac cilappatikārattum, maṇimēkaliyilum varutalāṉum,
"	amiccar purōkitar cēṉā patiyar
tavāttoḻiṟ ṟūtuvar cāraṇar eṉṟivar
pārttipark kimperuṅ kuḻuveṉap paṭumē"
"	karaṇat tiyalavar karuma vitikaḷ
kaṉakac cuṟṟam kaṭikāp pāḷar
nakara māntar naḷipaṭit talivar
yāṉi vīrar ivuḷi maṟavar
iṉiyar eṇpē rāyam eṉpa"
eṉat tivākaram mutaliya nikaṇṭukaḷ avaṟṟi viritturit talāṉum, piṟavāṟṟāṉum peṟappaṭum. uṟiyūrilum, kāvirip pūmpaṭṭiṉattilum kuṭikaḷiṉ vaḻakkukaḷikkēṭṭu nūṉeṟi piḻiyātu tīrppaḷikkum uyar nītimaṉṟaṅkaḷ (aṟaṅkūṟaviyam) iruntaṉa. ivaṟṟilirukkum nītipatikaḷ tarumācaṉattār eṉappaṭuvar.
"aṟaṅkeḻu nallavi uṟanti yaṉṉa"
eṉa akattilum,
"uṟanti aviyattu aṟam niṉṟu niliyiṟṟu"
eṉap puṟattilum,
"	aracukōl kōṭiṉum aṟaṅkū ṟaviyattu
urinūl kōṭi orutiṟam paṟṟiṉum"
eṉac cilappatikārattum varutal kāṇka. maṟṟum araciyal naṭāttutaṟku iṉṟiyamiyāta palatiṟappaṭṭa viṉiñarkaḷum iruntaṉarāvar eṉak koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
ivarkaḷ yāṉi, kutiri, tēr, kālāḷ eṉṉum nālvakip paṭiyum uṭiyarāyiruntaṉar. ivaṟṟi naṭāttum tāṉit talivar cōḻiya ēṉāti eṉṉum paṭṭam peṟutaṟkuriyarāvar. ivarkaḷ vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṉmītu pōrukkuc cellumpoḻutu veṟṟivāḷ, koṟṟak kuṭi, vīramuracu ivaṟṟi naṉ muḻuttattiṟ puṟappaṭac ceytal vaḻakkam. iṅṅaṉam ceytal vāṇāṭkōḷ, kuṭināṭkōḷ, muracunāṭkōḷ eṉappaṭum. ivar vañci cūṭip pakimēṟ cellumpoḻutu tam paṭiyāḷarkkup paṭikkalam mutaliyaṉavum, paricilarkkup poruḷum koṭuppar. pōriṉi mēṟkoṇṭa piṉṉāḷilē paṭittalivarkkum paṭikaṭkum peruviruntu ceytu makiḻvippar. itaṉi,
"piṇṭam mēya peruñcōṟṟu nili"
eṉpar tolkāppiyar. 'vēntaṉ pōrtalikkoṇṭa piṟṟi ñāṉṟu tāṉē pōr kuṟitta paṭiyāḷarum tāṉum uṭaṉuṇpāṉ pōlvatōr mukamaṉ ceytaṟkup piṇṭittu vitta uṇṭiyik koṭuttal mēyiṉa peruñ cōṟṟu nili' eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar. iṅṅaṉamāya pōr muṟikaḷ palavum tolkāppiyap puṟattiṇiyiyal mutaliyavaṟṟāṉ aṟiyap paṭum. ivi pōlvaṉa ēṉic cēra pāṇṭiya vēntarkaṭkum potuvāvaṉa eṉka. 
ivarkaḷ cēṉit talivarkku ēṉāti eṉṉum paṭṭam aḷittal pōṉṟē amiccar, kaṇakkar, vēḷāḷar mutalāyiṉārkkuk kāviti eṉṉum paṭṭamum, vaṇikarkku eṭṭi eṉṉum paṭṭamum aḷittu ataṟku aṭiyāḷamākap poṉṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa pūviṉi aḷippar. avi eṭṭippū, kāvitippū eṉappaṭum.
ivvēntarkaḷ piṟantanāḷ ovvōrāṇṭilum ciṟappākak koṇṭāṭappeṟum. appoḻutu ivarkaḷ maṅkala vaṇṇamākiya veḷḷaṇi aṇintu, cerucceytal, ciṟiceytal, koliceytal mutaliya ceṟṟac ceyalkaḷ ceyyātoḻintu, ciṟiviṭutal, iṟitavirtal, pulavar mutaliya takkārkku vēṇṭuvaṉa tarutal, iravalarkkītal mutaliya aṟac ceyalkaḷ ceyyā niṟpar. itu perumaṅkalam eṉavum, veḷḷaṇi eṉavum kūṟappaṭum. iṅṅaṉamē ivarkaḷ muṭipuṉinta nāḷtoṭaṅki ovvōrāṇṭiṉum muṭicūṭṭu nāḷum koṇṭāṭappeṟum. itu muṭipuṉintu nīrāṭutaliṉ maṇṇumaṅkalam eṉappaṭum. ivaṟṟi muṟiyē,
"	ciṟanta nāḷaṇi ceṟṟam nīkkip
piṟanta nāḷvayiṟ perumaṅkalamum"
"ciṟanta cīrtti maṇṇumaṅ kalamum"
eṉat tolkāppiyar kūṟutal kāṇka.


18. kittoḻil, vāṇikam
iṉi, annāḷilē cōṇāṭṭil kittoḻilkaḷum vāṇikamum eṅṅaṉam palkiyiruntaṉaveṉpatu karikālaṉ varalāṟu kūṟiya vaḻik kāṇappaṭum. aṅkē 'ariyavum periyavum' eṉac curuṅkak kūṟiyavaṟṟi iṅkē kāṭṭutum. cilappatikāram ūr kāṇkāti uriyil aṭiyārkku nallār kūṟiya arumaṇavaṉ, takkōli, kiṭāravaṉ, kārakil eṉappaṭṭa akiliṉ tokutiyum, kōcikam, pītakam, paccili, arattam, nuṇṭukil, cuṇṇam, vaṭakam, pañcu, iraṭṭu, pāṭakam, kōṅkalar, kōpam, cittirakkampi, kuruti, kariyal, pēṭakam, pariyaṭṭakkācu, vētaṅkam, puṅkarkkāḻakam, cilliki, tūriyam, paṅkam, tattiyam, vaṇṇaṭi, kavaṟṟumaṭi, nūlyāppu, tirukku, tēvāṅku, poṉṉeḻuttu, kuccari, tēvakiri, kāttūlam, iṟañci, veṇpotti, cempotti, paṇippotti eṉappaṭṭa tukiliṉ tokutiyum, maliyāram, tīmuraṇ pacci, kiḻāṉ pacci, pacciveṭṭi, aricantaṉam, vēr, cukkoṭi eṉappaṭṭa ārattokutiyum, amparēccam, kattūri, cavātu, cāntu, kuṅkumam, paṉinīr, puḻuku, takkōlam, nākappū, ilavaṅkam, cātikkāy, vacuvāci, niriyācam, tilam eṉappaṭṭa vācattokutiyum, maliccarakku, kali, aṭivucarakku, mārpu, iḷamārpu, ārūrkkāl, kiyoṭṭukkāl, mārppaṟṟu, vārācāṉ, kumaṭeṟivāṉ, urukkurukku, vāṟōcu, cūṭaṉ, cīṉaccūṭaṉ eṉappaṭṭa karuppūrattokutiyum pukār mutaliya nakaraṅkaḷil vāṇikam ceyyappaṭṭavātal orutali.


19. kalvi
immaṉṉarkaḷ kālattil araciyal mutaliya yāvum tamiḻ moḻi vāyilāka naṭipeṟṟu vantamiyālum, pāṇṭi nāṭṭilum cōṇāṭṭilum caṅkaṅkaḷ niṟuvit tamiḻ ārāyap peṟṟamiyālum, mūvēntarkaḷum vaḷḷalkaḷum variyāta vaṇmiyuṭaṉ pulavarkaḷi mika matittup pōṟṟivantami yālum tamiḻk kalviyāṉatu mika uyariyaniliyil iruntatu; pulavar pallāyiravar tikaḻntaṉar; iyal, ici, nāṭakam eṉṉum muttamiḻkkum ilakkaṇamākiya akattiyamum, tolkāppiyam, māpurāṇam, pūtapurāṇam, paṉampāram, paṉṉirupaṭalam, aviṉayam, kākkipāṭiṉiyam, ciṟu kākkipāṭiṉiyam, caṅkayāppu, naṟṟattam, palkāyam, mayēccuram, vāyppiyam, iṟiyaṉār kaḷaviyal mutaliya iyaṟṟamiḻ nūlkaḷum, perunāri, peruṅkuruku, icinuṇukkam, peruṅkalam, intirakāḷiyam, pañcapāratīyam, pañcamarapu mutaliya icittamiḻ nūlkaḷum, paratam, kuṇanūl, kūttanūl, muṟuval, cayantam, ceyiṟṟiyam, parata cēṉāpatīyam, mativāṇaṉār nāṭakattamiḻnūl mutaliya nāṭakattamiḻ nūl kaḷum taḻittu viḷaṅkiṉa; eṇṇiṟanta paripāṭal, mutunāri, mutukuruku, kaḷariyāviri uḷḷiṭṭaṉa taliccaṅkattārālum, kali, kuruku, veṇṭāḷi, viyāḻa māliyakaval eṉa ittoṭak kattaṉa iṭic caṅkattārālum, neṭuntoki nāṉūṟu, kuṟuntoki nāṉūṟu, naṟṟiṇi nāṉūṟu, puṟanāṉūṟu, aiṅkuṟu nūṟu, patiṟṟuppattu, nūṟṟimpatukali, eḻupatu paripāṭal, pattuppāṭṭu, muttoḷḷāyiram, kūttu, vari, ciṟṟici, pērici eṉṟu ittoṭakkattaṉa kaṭic caṅkattārālum pāṭappeṟṟu nilaviṉa; teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuva tēvarāl tirukkuṟaḷ eṉṉum centamiḻt tirumaṟiyum, cēramuṉiyākiya iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷāl cilappatikāramum, kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉārāl maṇimēkaliyum, kapilar mutaliya nallicip pulavarkaḷāl iṉṉā nāṟpatu mutaliya ēṉiya kīḻkkaṇakku nūlkaḷum iyaṟṟappeṟṟu oḷirntaṉa; vāṉanūl, eṇṇūl, maruttunūl mutaliya kalikaḷum vaḻakkil iruntaṉa. maṟṟum, akamuḻavu, akappuṟa muḻavu, puṟamuḻavu, puṟappuṟa muḻavu, paṇṇami muḻavu, nāṇmuḻavu, kālimuḻavu eṉa ēḻuvakiyāṟ pakukkap paṭum pēriki, paṭakam, iṭakki, uṭukki, mattaḷam, calliki, karaṭiki, timili, kuṭamuḻā, takki, kaṇappaṟi, tamarukam, taṇṇumi, taṭāri, antari, muḻavu, cantiravaḷiyam, monti, muracu, kaṇviṭutūmpu, nicālam, tuṭumi, ciṟupaṟi, aṭakkam, takuṇiccam, viralēṟu, pākam, upāṅkam, nāḻikippaṟi, tuṭi, perumpaṟi mutaliya tōṟkaruvikaḷum, vaṅkiyam eṉṉum tuḷikkaruviyum, pēriyāḻ, makarayāḻ, cakōṭayāḻ, ceṅkōṭṭiyāḻ eṉṉum narappukkaruvikaḷum, kañcakkaruviyum payilap peṟṟu vantaṉa; āyiram narampuṭiya ātiyāḻ eṉpatum iruntatu; kural, tuttam, kikkiḷi, uḻi, iḷi, viḷari, tāram eṉṉum ēḻiciyāṉum piṟakkum paṇ vikaṟpaṅkaḷum, patiṉōrāyirattut toḷḷāyirattut toṇṇūṟ ṟoṉṟākiya ātiyicikaḷum tamiḻppāṇarkaḷāl aṟintu pāṭappeṟṟu vantaṉa; vēttiyal, potuviyal eṉṉum irutiṟa muṭiya akakkūttu, puṟakkūttukaḷum, kuravi, kalinaṭam, kuṭak kūttu, karaṇam, nōkku, tōṟpāvi eṉṉum vinōtak kūttu
kaḷum, ammaṉi, pantu, kaḻaṅkāṭal, unti, cāḻal, pallāṅkuḻi, avaliṭi, koṟṟi, picci, vāricci, cintuppiḻukki, kuṭappiḻukki, pāṇṭip piḻukki, pāmpāṭṭi, ālaṅkāṭṭāṇṭi mutaliya palvarik kūttukaḷum, piṟavum naṭikkappeṟṟu vantaṉa.
ippoḻutu veḷivantuḷḷa eṭṭuttoki, pattuppāṭṭu mutaliya kaṭiccaṅka ilakkiyaṅkaḷiliruntu aṟiyappaṭum pulavar ēṟakkuṟiya eḻunūṟṟuvar āvar. ivarkaḷil oḷaviyār, ātimantiyār, kākkipāṭiṉiyār nacceḷḷiyār, okkūr mācāttiyār, veḷḷivītiyār, veṇṇik kuyattiyār, kuṟamakaḷ iḷaveyiṉi, kuṟamakaḷ kuṟiyeyiṉi, kāvaṟpeṇṭu, kuṉṟiyāḷ, kaḻārkkīraṉ eyiṟṟiyār, kāmakkaṇip pacaliyār, nakkaṇṇiyār, naṉṉākiyār, pūṅkaṇṇuttiriyār, poṉmaṇiyār, māṟōkattu nappacaliyār, pōntip pacaliyār, aḷḷūr naṉmulliyār, pārimakaḷir mutalāyiṉār nallicip pulami vāynta melliyalārāvar. ivaṟṟiliruntu akkālattē kalviyil irupālārum eṅṅaṉam ciṟappaṭintiruntaṉa reṉpatu pulappaṭum.


20. camayam
tamiḻ makkaḷ pallāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpē uyir uṭalkaḷi vevvēṟāka uṇarntiruntaṉar eṉpatu tamiḻ eḻuttukkaḷukku uyir eṉṟum mey eṉṟum peyariṭṭirut talāṉē peṟappaṭum. evvaḷavu paḻaṅkālattiṟ ceṉṟu nōkkiṉum tamiḻarkaḷ ulakam, uyir, kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum mūṉṟu poruḷiyum uṇarntiruntamiyōṭu, ulakam aimpūtaṅkaḷālāyateṉṟum, uyirkaḷ viṉik kaṭṭuṭiyaṉa eṉṟum, kaṭavuḷ viṉik kaṭṭil lātavar eṉṟum aṟintiruntaṉar eṉpatu pulaṉākiṉṟatu. mikka paḻami vāyntatākiya tolkāppiyattil,
"	kālam ulakam uyirē uṭampē
pālvari teyvam viṉiyē pūtam"
eṉavum,
"	nilantī nīrvaḷi vicumpō ṭintum
kalanta mayakkam ulakam"
eṉavum, 
"	viṉiyiṉ nīṅki viḷaṅkiya aṟiviṉ
muṉivaṉ"
eṉavum kūṟappaṭṭiruttal kāṇka. maṟṟum atilē kaṭavuḷ eṉṉum peyarum, kaṭavuḷi vāḻttutalum kūṟiyiruppatiṉiṉṟu paramporuḷāṉatu ellāvaṟṟiyum kaṭantu niṟkum iyalpiṉa teṉpatum, ataṉi niṉintu vāḻtti vaḻipaṭutalē uyirkaḷ kaṭṭiṉiṉṟum nīṅki iṉpuṟutaṟkuriya neṟi eṉpatum avarkaḷ aṟintiruntami pōtarum. iyalpākavē kaṭṭiṉmiyum, muṟṟuṇarvum, varampiliṉpamum uṭiya am meyp poruḷiyē palvēṟu kālaṅkaḷil paṟpala tēyattiṉumuḷḷa māntarkaḷ tāmtām aṟintavāṟu vevvēṟu peyarāṉum, vevvēṟu neṟiyāṉum vaḻipaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉar.tamiḻ nāṭṭilē kuṟiñcinila makkaṭku murukakkaṭavuḷum, mullinila makkaṭkut tirumālum, marutanila makkaṭku intiraṉum, neytal nila makkaṭku varuṇaṉum teyvaṅkaḷ eṉat tamiḻ nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tiṇippirivum, tiṇimakkaṭ pirivum iyaṟki yāṉaviyē eṉiṉum, tiṇikaṭkut teyva aṭivu ēṟakkuṟiya nālāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉpu ēṟpaṭṭirukka vēṇṭumeṉat tōṉṟukiṟatu. kaṇṇapirāṉ āyarpāṭiyil vaḷarntu āyar kaḷuṭaṉ cērntu āviṉam mēyttal mutaliya viḷiyāṭalkaḷic ceytu pōnta piṉṉarē mulli makkaḷiṭattu māyōṉ vaḻipāṭu ciṟantu viḷaṅkiṟṟu eṉṉalām. āriyavētaṅkaḷil mikuttuk kūṟappaṭum intiraṉ vaḻipāṭum, varuṇaṉ vaḻipāṭum akkālattiṟṟāṉ tamiḻarkaḷālē kikkoḷḷappaṭṭirukka vēṇṭum. murukaṉ vaḻipāṭu aṅṅaṉam iṭiyil uṇṭāya teṉa niṉikkak kāraṇamilli.
iṉi, paṇṭit tamiḻ ilakkiyaṅkaḷil ciṟappākak kūṟap paṭum kaṭavuḷar nālvar. nakkīrar pāṭiya 56-ām puṟappāṭṭilē iṭapakkoṭiyum kaṇiccippaṭiyum maṇimiṭaṟum uṭiya civaperumāṉum, vālvaḷimēṉiyum kalappippaṭiyum paṉikkoṭiyum uṭiya palarāmaṉum, nīlamaṇiyokkum mēṉiyum kaluḻakkoṭiyum uṭiya kaṇṇaṉum, mayilākiya koṭiyum mayilūrtiyum uṭiya murukavēḷum muṟiyē kūṟappeṟṟuḷar. atil,
"	ñālaṅ kākkum kāla muṉpiṉ
tōlā nallici nālvar"
eṉa ivarkaḷ ulakattip purappōrākak kūṟiyiruppatum cintikkaṟpālatu. kapilar pāṭiya iṉṉānāṟpatilum,
"	mukkaṭ pakavaṉ aṭitoḻā tārkkiṉṉā
poṟpaṉi veḷḷiyi uḷḷā toḻukiṉṉā
cakkarat tāṉi maṟappiṉṉā āṅkiṉṉā
cattiyāṉ ṟāṭoḻā tārkku"
eṉa innālvarum immuṟiyē kūṟappeṟṟaṉar. cilappatikāram intiraviḻavūreṭutta kātiyil,
"	piṟavā yākkip periyōṉ kōyilum
aṟumukac cevvēḷ aṇitikaḻ kōyilum
vālvaḷi mēṉi vāliyōṉ kōyilum
nīla mēṉi neṭiyōṉ kōyilum"
eṉavum, ūrkāṇ kātiyil,
"	nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟiyōṉ kōyilum
uvaṇac cēval uyarttōṉ niyamamum
mēḻivalaṉ uyartta veḷḷi nakaramum
kōḻic cēvaṟ koṭiyōṉ kōṭṭamum"
eṉavum kāvirippūmpaṭṭiṉattilum, maturiyilum innāṉku tēvarkaḷiṉ kōyilkaḷ iruntaṉavāka muṟiyē kūṟap peṟṟuḷa. ivaṟṟil nālvariṉ muṟi muṟkūṟiyavāṟaṉṟic ciṟitu piṟaḻntuḷatu. eṉiṉum, yāṇṭum civaperumāṉē mutalil vittuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. aṉṟiyum, avar, piṟavāyākkip periyōṉ eṉavum, pakavaṉ eṉavum iṟiyōṉ eṉavum talimi tōṉṟak kūṟappaṭutalum cintikkaṟpālatu. vēṟiṭaṅ kaḷil iṉṉum veḷippaṭiyākavum civapirāṉukku mutaṉmi kūṟappeṟṟuḷatu.
"	nīrum nilaṉum tīyum vaḷiyum
māka vicumpō ṭintuṭa ṉiyaṟṟiya
maḻuvāḷ neṭiyōṉ taliva ṉāka
mācaṟa viḷaṅkiya yākkiyar"
eṉa māṅkuṭi marutaṉār maturikkāñciyilum,
"	nutalviḻi nāṭṭat tiṟiyōṉ mutalāp
pativāḻ catukkattut teyvamī ṟāka"
eṉak kūlavāṇikaṉ cāttaṉār maṇimēkaliyilum kūṟiyiruttal nōkkuka. ivvāṟṟāl, toṉṟutoṭṭut tamiḻmakkaḷ muḻumutaṟ kaṭavuḷākakkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭṭu vanta teyvam civaperumāṉē eṉpatu veḷḷiṭi malipōl teḷḷitiṉ viḷaṅkum. piṟkālattuc camaṇavāciriyar mutalāyiṉārum tamiḻ vaḻakkiṉ ivvuṇmiyuṇarntē tam nūlkaḷil pala kaṭavuḷariyum kūṟumiṭattuc civaperumāṉi mutaṟkaṇ vittuk kūṟip pōntaṉar. nacciṉārkkiṉiyar, aṭiyārkkunallār mutaliya uriyāciriyaṉmārum inneṟi piḻiyāmalē urivari vārāyiṉar. iṅṅaṉamāyiṉum, paṇṭit tamiḻ makkaḷ tirumāliṭattum cevvēḷiṭattum niṟinta aṉpuṭiya rākavē viḷaṅkiṉar. iṉṉōriyum ulakaṅkākkum kaṭavuḷa rākap pōṟṟip pukaḻntaṉarē yaṉṟi oru ciṟitum kuṟivupaṭak kūṟiṉārallar. maṟṟum, toki nūlkaḷiṉ kaṭavuḷ vāḻttilum, paripāṭalilum civapirāṉōṭu vēṟṟumiyillāta mutaṟ ṟeyvaṅkaḷākavē ivarkaḷ paravappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. iṉṉum, civa vaḻi pāṭṭiṉum cevvēḷ vaḻipāṭu viñciyirunta teṉak kūṟutalum cālum. pattuppāṭṭil oru pāṭṭum (tirumurukāṟṟup paṭiyum), paripāṭalil muppattoru pāṭṭum murukap pirāṉukku uriyavāyiruttalē ivvuṇmiyi viḷakkum; murukavēḷ eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum tiruppatikaḷ pala caṅkac ceyyuṭkaḷiṟ payiṉṟiruttalum ataṟkuc cāṉṟākum. tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, tiruccīralivāy, tiruvāviṉaṉkuṭi, tiruvērakam mutaliya tiruppatikaḷit tirumurukāṟṟup paṭi kūṟukiṟatu. paripāṭalum, piṟa tokikaḷum avaṟṟip pukaḻnturikkiṉṟaṉa; cilappatikāramum,
"	cīrkeḻu centilum 1ceṅkōṭum veṇkuṉṟum  ērakamum nīṅkā iṟivaṉ"
eṉṟēttukiṉṟatu.
iṉi, paḻiya tamiḻ makkaḷiṉ camayakkōṭpāṭu ituveṉak kūṟavē cōḻarkaḷiṉ camayamum ittaṉmit teṉpatu kūṟāmalē viḷaṅkum. cōḻamaṉṉarkaḷ civaperumāṉiyē mutaṟkaṭavuḷākak koṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvōr eṉpatum, tirumāliṭattum cevvēḷiṭattum pēraṉpuṭiyār eṉpatum kaṭippiṭikka. karikālvaḷavaṉ, kōcceṅkaṇṇar eṉṉum cōḻa vēntarkaḷ paṟpala civālayat tiruppaṇikaḷ ceytu pōntamiyum avarkaḷ camayam civam eṉpataṉi nili niṟuttum. cōḻarkaḷ intiraṉukkup periyatōr tiruviḻāc
ceytu pōntatiliruntu avarkaḷ nāṭṭiṉ nalaṅkaruti intiraṉiyum aṉpuṭaṉ pūcittu vantaṉareṉpatu peṟappaṭum. avarkaḷ intiraṉip peruñ ciṟappuṭaṉ koṇṭāṭi vanta kāraṇattāl avaṉuṭiya kaṟpakattaru, veḷḷiyāṉi, vaccirappaṭi eṉpavaṟṟiṟkum pukār nakarilē taṉittaṉiyākak kōyilkaḷ ēṟpaṭuvaṉa vāyiṉa.
iṉi, vaṭa nāṭṭiṟ ceṅkōlōcciya peruvēntaṉākiya acōkaṉ taṉ makkaḷākiya caṅkamittiri, makēntu eṉpavar kaḷuṭaṉ puttamata pōtakar palarit tēyantōṟum viṭuttu am matattip paravac ceytamiyāl, ki. mu. mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭilēyē puttamatam tamiḻ nāṭṭilum iṭam peṟṟu vaḷarat toṭaṅ kiṟṟu. camaṇa matamum akkālantoṭaṅkiyē paravaluṟṟatu eṉṉalām. cilappatikāram mutaliyaṉa koṇṭu nōkkumiṭattu, ki.pi. mutal, iraṇṭām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷilē, tamiḻ nāṭṭiṉ tali nakaraṅkaḷākiya pukār, uṟanti, maturi, vañci, kāñci eṉṉum iṭaṅkaḷil camaṇa putta mataṅkaḷ mikka valimi yuṟṟiruntami pulaṉākiṉṟatu. arukaṉ kōṭṭamum, putta cittiyamum nakarppuṟaṅkaḷil kaṭṭap paṭṭiruntaṉa; camaṇa muṉivarkaḷum, puttamuṉivarkaḷum uṟiyum paḷḷikaḷ āṅkāṅkiruntaṉa; cāraṇarkaḷ iṭiyaṟātu matapōtaṉi purintu vantārkaḷ; āṭavarkaḷum  peṇṭirkaḷum mikutiyāka ammataṅkaḷiṟ pukuvārāyiṉar. akkālattut tamiḻ vēntarkaḷ naṭuvuniliyuṭaṉ ittaṉikkum iṭantantu avarkaḷiyum purantu vantami peritum pārāṭṭaṟ kuriyatu. civaṉ, tirumāl mutaliya teyvaṅkaḷi eḷḷutaliṉṟiyē avarkaḷ tam mataṅkaḷip pōtittu vantamiyāṟṟāṉ avi virintu paravaluṟṟaṉa eṉakkarutal poruntum.
iṉi, akkālattiluḷḷa pulavarkaḷāyiṉum piṟarāyiṉum avarkaḷ tammiṉ vēṟupaṭṭa camayattārkaḷiṉ uṇarcciyi matittu avaṟṟic ciṟitum ikaḻātu pārāṭṭi vantaṉar eṉpatum, ōr kuṭumpattiluḷḷa palar tattam viruppattiṟ kiyintavāṟu vevvēṟu mataṅkaḷik kikkoṇṭum tammuḷ vēṟṟumi ciṟitumiṉṟi oḻuki vantaṉar eṉpatum paḻiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷi ṉiṉṟum aṟiyalākiṉṟa uṇmikaḷām. eccamayattiyum ikaḻātu pōṟṟum peruntaṉmiyuṭimikkut teyvappulamit tiruvaḷḷuvaṉāriyum iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷiyum ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭākak koḷḷal takum. vaḷḷuvaṉār,
"	nūlōr tokuttavaṟṟu ḷellām tali"
"pallāṟṟāl tēriṉu maḵtē tuṇi"
eṉṟiṅṅaṉam ellā nūliṉum nallaṉa eṭuttu ellārkkum potuppaṭak kūṟic celvar; em matattiyum ikaḻār.
iḷaṅkōvaṭikaḷ, tam tamiyaṉākiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ civaperumāṉ tiruvaruḷāṟ piṟantavaṉ eṉṟum, avaṉ imayañ cellap puṟappaṭṭapoḻutu ulakupotiyuruvat tuyarntōṉākiya civaperumāṉ cēvaṭiyi muṭimēṟ koṇṭu yāriyum iṟiñcāc ceṉṉiyāl iṟiñci valaṅkoṇṭu pōntu yāṉiyiṉ piṭaril ēṟiyaruḷi piṉpu āṭakamāṭattu aṟituyilamarnta tirumāliṉ cēṭaṅ koṇṭuvantu cilar niṉṟu tutittakāliyil tāṉ civaperumāṉ tiruvaṭiyi maṇimuṭi mēl vittiruttaliṉ ataṉi vāṅkit tōḷiṉmītu tarittaṉaṉ eṉṟum kūṟumukattāl ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ civaperumāṉiyē muḻumutalākakkoṇṭu vaḻipaṭuvōṉ eṉpatu peṟavittu, tamkoḷkiyum atuvātali 'piṟavāyākkip periyōṉ' eṉpatu mutaliyavaṟṟāṟ kuṟippiṭṭaṉar.  aṉṉarāyiṉum, avar, kavuntiyaṭikaḷ aruka tēvaṉi vāḻttutal kūṟumiṭattu,
"	orumūṉ ṟavittōṉ ōtiya ñāṉat
tirumoḻik kallateṉ ceviyakam tiṟavā
kāmaṉi veṉṟōṉ āyirat teṭṭu
nāmam allatu navilā teṉṉā
aivari veṉṟōṉ aṭiyiṇi yallatu
kivarik kāṇiṉum kāṇā eṉ kaṇ
aruḷaṟam pūṇṭōṉ tirumeyk kallateṉ
poruḷil yākki pūmiyiṟ poruntātu"
eṉṟiṅṅaṉamum;
vēṭṭuvavariyil avarkaḷ koṟṟaviyip paravumiṭattu,
"	amari kumari kavuri camari
cūli nīli mālavaṟ kiḷaṅkiḷi
aiyi ceyyavaḷ veyyavāṭ ṭaṭakkip
pāykalip pāvi pintoṭip pāvi
āykalip pāvi aruṅkalap pāvi"
"	āṉittōl pōrttup puliyi ṉuriyuṭuttuk
kāṉat terumik karuntalimēl niṉṟāyāl
vāṉōr vaṇaṅka maṟimēṉ maṟiyāki
ñāṉak koḻuntāy naṭukkiṉṟi yēniṟpāy"
eṉṟu ivvāṟākavum;
āycciyar kuraviyil avarkaḷ tirumālip paravumiṭattu,
"	mūvulakum īraṭiyāl muṟinirampā vakimuṭiyat
tāviyacē vaṭicēppat tampiyoṭuṅ kāṉpōntu
cōvaraṇum pōrmaṭiyat tollilaṅki kaṭṭaḻitta
cēvakaṉcīr kēḷāta ceviyeṉṉa ceviyē
tirumālcīr kēḷāta ceviyeṉṉa ceviyē"
"	periyavaṉi māyavaṉip pērulaka mellām
virikamala vuntiyuṭi viṇṇavaṉik kaṇṇum
tiruvaṭiyum kiyum tiruvāyum ceyya
kariyavaṉik kāṇāta kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇē
kaṇṇimittuk kāṇpār tam kaṇṇeṉṉa kaṇṇē"
eṉṟu ittaṉmiyākavum;
kuṉṟakkuraviyil kuṟavarkaḷ murukavēḷip pāṭumiṭattu,
"	cīrkeḻu centilum ceṅkōṭum veṇkuṉṟum
ērakamum nīṅkā iṟivaṉki vēlaṉṟē
pārirum puvatti ṉuḷpukkup paṇṭorunāḷ
cūrmā taṭinta cuṭariliya veḷvēlē"
"	aṇimukaṅka ḷōrāṟum īrāṟu kiyum
iṇiyiṉṟit tāṉuṭiyāṉ ēntiyavē laṉṟē
piṇimukamēṟ koṇṭavuṇar pīṭaḻiyum vaṇṇam
maṇivicumpiṟ kōṉētta māṟaṭṭa veḷvēlē"
eṉṟu iṉṉaṇamum; avaravar uṇarcciyuṭaṉ kalantu ellāt teyvaṅkaḷiyum vāyāra maṉamāra vaḻuttuvārāyiṉar.
iṉi, civapirāṉ tirukkōyil mutalāka muṉ kūṟippōnta kōyilkaḷaṉṟi, mācāttaṉ, ñāyiṟu, tiṅkaḷ eṉṉum teyvaṅ kaṭkum, tirukkili, cevvēḷiṉ kivēl eṉpavaṟṟukkum taṉittaṉiyāka iyaṟṟappeṟṟa kōyilkaḷ annāḷilē nilaviṉa veṉka.


kuṟippuri
mukavuri :  mūvar - mūvēntar. poḻil - ulakam. nūkka - taḷḷa. tuṟakkam-cuvarkkam. akattu uḻiñiyāṉ-pakivar matili muṟṟukiyiṭṭa poḻutu uḷḷiruntu matilip pātukāppōṉ.
pa.147.	tekkaṇam-teṉṟēyam; vintiyamalikkut teṟkiluḷḷa nāṭu. nilikkaḷaṉ - iṭam. 
pa.148.	alaṅku-aciyum. uḷi-kutiriyiṉ piṭari mayir; kutiriyiṉ taliyāṭṭam eṉṉum aṇi. aivar-pāṇṭavar. ciṉii-kōpittu. polam-poṉ. īrimpatiṉmar-turiyōtaṉaṉ mutaliya nūṟṟuvar. patam-uṇavu. cēy-murukaṉ pōlum aracaṉ.
pa.142.	kapāṭapuram-iṭiccaṅkam irunta nakaram. 
pa.149.	vaḻaṅkuvatu - koṭuttaṟkuriya poruḷ. uḷvīḻntak kaṇṇum-kuṟinta 		viṭattum. talippirital - nīṅkutal.
pa.150. 	kōl nilitirintu-aracaṉ ceṅkōl kōṇutaluṟṟu. kaḻumiya - niṟinta. kavāṉ-toṭi.
pa.151. 	patieḻuaṟiyā-patiyiṉiṉṟum peyartal aṟiyāta. karuvaṉāy - cerukkuṭiyavaṉāy; kāraṇamāṉavaṉāki eṉṟumām. maruvaṉāy - maṇam pōlvāṉāki.
pa.153.	kañcam-nīr. kāntamaṉ-kāntaṉākiya aracaṉ. ceṅkaṇ āyirattōṉ-intiraṉ. āram pūṇṭōṉ-pāṇṭiyaṉ.
pa.156. 	kaḻuvāy - pirāyaccittam. tūṅkiyatu-toṅkiyatu. tuṇukkuṟṟu-tiṭukkiṭṭu. 
pa.157.	cūḻvuṟa - ālōcikka. uñaṟṟutal-ceytal.
pa.158. 	āviṉ kaṭimaṇi-pacuviṉ kaṭikkaṇ. 
pa.159.	viñciyaṉ - vittiyātaraṉ.
pa.160. 	mucu - kuraṅkiṉ oruvaki. mucukkali - āṇ kuraṅku.
pa.161. 	viciṭṭam-mēṉmi.
pa.162. 	veḷḷiviṇṭu - veḷḷimali. ikkuvēḷ-karumpu villiyuṭiya maṉmataṉ. āriyi-umātēviyār. kūviḷam-vilvam. kuyiṟṟiya-ceyta. vēli culāvum-kaṭal cūḻnta. mēkavākaṉaṉ - intiraṉ. aṉikam - cēṉi. cīkaram - nīrttuḷi.
pa.163.  	āral - kārttiki. tuliyām tiṅkaḷ-aippaci mātam. arutti-viruppam. oṉṉalar-pakivar.
pa.165. 	koṭuvari-vaḷinta varikaḷiyuṭiyatu; puli.
pa.166. 	viḻākkōḷ-tiruviḻāc ceytal.
pa.169.	vāṉa ūrti - teyva vimāṉam. puṉkaṇ - tuṉpam.
pa.170.	alamaral - varuttam. kaṉali-cūriyaṉ. kāl - kāṟṟu. koṭkum - tiriyum. maruḷa-viyakka. ēṟu - eṟṟutal. taputi-aḻivu. cīri-tulām. marukaṉ-cantatiyil vantōṉ.
pa.173. 	kuṭakakkuvaṭu - kuṭakamali. maḻuvāḷneṭiyōṉ - paracurāmaṉ. kaṉṉi-turkki. kūuy - aḻittu. uḷvarik koṇṭu - vēṟṟurukkoṇṭu.
pa.174. 	uṭkum - añcum. ūṅkaṇōr - muṉṉōr. mēmpaṭīiya - mēmpaṭutta. 
pa.175.	urumu - iṭi. cuval - tōḷ.

1. 	tolkāppiyari jamatakkiṉi muṉivariṉ puttirareṉa nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟukiṉṟār; jamatakkiṉiyippaṟṟi yeḻunta purāṇap pakutikaḷil aṅṅaṉam kūṟappaṭāmiyāṉum, ēṉit tamiḻ nūl uriyāciriyarkaḷum kūṟiṟṟilarākalāṉum nacciṉārkkiṉiyar kūṟṟut teruḷaṟpālataṉṟu. avaruriyē paṟṟi yeḻunta piṉṉuḷōr kūṟṟukkaḷum aṉṉa.
1. 	tiruviḷiyāṭaṟ purāṇattu akattiyaṉār nakkīraṉārkku ilakkaṇamupatēcittā
reṉak kūṟum kūṟṟu carita vārāyccikku ottataṉṟu. tavayōkam kivarappeṟṟa amaramuṉiyākiya akattiyar ēṉic cāmāṉiya maṉittarpōlātu paṉṉeṭuṅ kālam uyirvāḻntiruttal kūṭumeṉavum, avar iṟṟikkum nammaṉōraṟiyāvāṟu yāṇṭēṉum iruttal amiyumeṉavum koḷvē meṉiṉ ataṉi maṟukka yāṉ muṉvantilēṉeṉka.
1. " maṉmaruṅ kaṟutta maḻuvā ṇeṭiyōṉ
ṟaṉmuṉ ṟōṉṟal takātoḻi nīyeṉak
kaṉṉi yēvaliṟ kānta maṉṉavaṉ
innakar kāppōr yāreṉa niṉii
nāvalan taṇpoḻi ṉaṇṇār naṭukkuṟak
kāvaṟ kaṇiki taṉakkāṅ kātalaṉ
ikaḻntōr kāyiṉu meñcuta lillōṉ 
kakanta ṉāmeṉak kātaliṟ kūuy
aracā ḷurimi niṉpā liṉmiyiṟ
paracu rāmaṉiṉ pālvan taṇukāṉ
amara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ ṟaṉātu
tuyarnīṅku kiḷaviyiṉ yāṉṟōṉ ṟaḷavuṅ
kakantaṉ kāttal'
- maṇimēkali, ciṟicey kāti 25-37.
2. ‘ ceṅkatirc celvaṉ ṟirukkulam viḷakkuṅ
kañca vēṭkiyiṟ kāntamaṉ vēṇṭa
vamara muṉivaṉ akattiyaṉ ṟaṉātu
karakaṅ - kaviḻtta kāvirip pāvi'
- maṇimēkali, patikam 9-12.

5. ‘ munnīr viḻavi ṉeṭiyōṉ
naṉṉīrp paḵṟuḷi maṇaliṉum palavē' - puṟam 9.
1. ‘	vaṭivē leṟinta vāṉpaki poṟātu
paḵṟuḷi yāṟṟuṭaṉ paṉmali yaṭukkattuk
kumarikkōṭum koṭuṅkaṭal koḷḷa'
- cilappatikāram, kāṭukāṇ kāti 18-20.

kiḻami,  pūca nakṣattiram, piratami titiyil eḻutappaṭṭatāka atiliruntu terikiṟatu.

1.	*iṅkē kūṟiya varalāṟukaḷip piṉvarum pāṭalkaḷ curukkamāka uṇarttuvaṉa:
"	mikka viṉṉatu niṉṟiṭa muṉṉam
		veḷḷi viṇṭuvil viṇṭoṭu kāvil
	ikku vēḷi yeritta nutaṟkaṇ
		empi rāṉumi yōṭu miruppa
	akka ṇattiṉ mucukkali yoṉṟaṅ
		kāti mītiṉum āriyi mītum
	takka villuva nallili cintat
		tiyal cīṟalum aiyaṉ vilakki."
"	kūvi ḷattaḻi koṇṭemi yiṅṅaṉ
		kōtil pūci kuyiṟṟiya paṇpāl
	nīva ḷintiṭa vēli culāvum
		nīḷpu vikkara cākuti yeṉṟē
	māva ḷikki yiṭatta ṉicippa
		viya kattiṭi vantu piṟantē
	kāva ḷitta vayaṟkaru  vūrvāḻ
		kāmar cērmucu kunta ṉiṭattiṉ."
"	mēka vākaṉaṉ oṟṟar paṭarntē
		veṉṟi vēlavaṉ maṉṟal viḷampa
	ōki yeyti yeḻuntaṉi kattō
		ṭumpar nāta ṉuṟuṅkiri yuṟṟāṉ
	cīka ramceṟi cākara maṇṇiṟ
		cēru maṉṉavar yāru maṭintār
	māka nēriṭi mātarkaṇ mintar
		vāvi kāvi lulāvi makiḻntār."	
"	kūṟitu niṉṟiṭa vēṟoru nāḷmucu
		kunta ṉeṉuntalivaṉ
	māṟuta laṟṟa viciṭṭa maṭinta
		vaciṭṭaṉi vantupaṇin
	tāṟu muṭitta varuṭkaṭa ṉalkiya
		āṟu muṭipperumaḷāṉ
vīṟu pukaḻppeṟu virata murittaru
		ḷeṉṟu viḷamputalum."
"	veḷḷi yeṉuntiṉam ārala taṉṟiyum
		veṉṟi yuṟuntuliyām
	oḷḷiya tiṅkaḷi lintu vaḷarntiṭa
		vōṅkumuṉ vikalmuṉā 
	eḷḷari tāmaṟu nāḷu meḻiṟpara
		cēntiya vēntaltarum
vaḷḷal makiḻntaruḷ virata meṉappukaḻ
		mātava ṉōtiyarō."
"	aṉṉa tiyaṟṟalum aṉṉatu taṉṉi 
		larutti poruttimuṭit
	tuṉṉaru mēṉmi yaṭintavar tammiyu
		mōtiṉaṉ ōtutalum
	iṉṉalil maṉṉava ṉappaṭi paṟpaka
		lippuvi yiṟpuriyā
	oṉṉalar vantu paṇintiṭa viya
		muvantu purantaṉaṉē"
"	mēvoru nāḷtaṉiṉ maṇṇula kattiṉum
		viṇṇulakat taṇukit
	tēvar vaṇaṅkuṟu kōvi varuttu
		tirukkavu ṇappaṭiveṉ
	ṟōvil perumpukaḻ ēḻu viṭaṅkari
		yoṇpuvi mītukoṇarn
	tēvaru maṉpoṭu kāṇa virutti
		yiruṅkati yuṟṟaṉaṉē."
(kantapurāṇac carukkam, tēvakāṇṭam 12, 13, 14, takṣakāṇṭam 178, 179, 180, 181.)
1.	*srīmat M. V. irāmaṉujāccāriyār avarkaḷ patippitta viyācapārata moḻipeyarppuk kāṇka.
1.	" 	maṉ maruṅ kaṟutta maḻuvā ṇeṭiyōṉ
taṉmuṉ ṟōṉṟal takātoḻi nīyeṉak
kaṉṉiyēvaliṟ kānta maṉṉavaṉ

iyinta nāmam ippatikkiṭ ṭīṅku
uḷvarik koṇṭav vuravōṉ peyarnāḷ"
				(maṇimēkali 22:25-39)

kāviri koṇṭoḷit tāṅku"
 					 		(akam, 376.)
1.	paḻiya kālattil iṟantavar uṭalit tāḻiyāṟkavittu aṭakkam ceytal vaḻakkam; itu mutumakkaṭṭāḻi eṉappaṭum.
1.	cēraṉ ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉ pakkam 102, 103.
1.*"	puṇarcci paḻakutal vēṇṭā vuṇarccitāṉ
naṭpāṅ kiḻami tarum"
eṉṉum kuṟaḷuriyil, ‘kōpperuñ cōḻaṉukkum picirāntiyārkkum pōla uṇarcci yoppiṉ atuvē uṭaṉuyirnīṅkum urimittāya naṭpiṉip payakku meṉpatām' eṉapparimēlaḻakar urittalum kāṇka.
1.	tamiḻakattiluḷḷa nāṭukaḷiṉ pirivuvakikaḷ yāṉeḻutiya kaḷḷar carittiram eṉṉum puttakattil virivāka ārāyntu kāṭṭap peṟṟuḷḷaṉa.
1.	ceṅkōṭu eṉpatu tirucceṅkōṭu eṉṟum, veṇkuṉṟam eṉpatu cuvāmimali eṉṟum kūṟuvar cilappatikāra arumpata uriyāciriyar. ērakam malināṭṭakattoru tiruppati eṉpar nacciṉārkkiṉiyar.
